### F-094 [S3, unverified] FP exceptions and uninitialised reads in dead or discarded computations abort runtime-checked builds (RSA z/dz with evolve_delta_phi, quintessence drag IC y(2), Beyond-Horndeski hpprime)

- **Location:** `fortran/equations.f90:3008` (also: fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p4_quintessence.f90:707-711, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p5_Beyond_Horndeski.f90:1171)
- **Track:** t1-crash; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** (a) 5_hdsk_TG_1/3 (EFTCAMB_evolve_delta_phi = T): FP trap at equations.f90:3010 (z = 1/EFTeomG*... with EFTeomG = 0, coefficients never computed on that path); with the dead statements removed both complete under -fcheck=all + traps in 62/59 s. (b) 5_quint_axion, 5_quint_exponential: trap at 008p4_quintessence.f90:859/870 because derivs is called with y(2) uninitialised (drag_initial_conditions, line 710); the quantities used afterwards do not depend on y(2). (c) 008p5_Beyond_Horndeski.f90:1171 computes x3ppp from never-assigned hpprime (-Wuninitialized); the value is recomputed at 1208 before use (dead store). All three fixes together (relS) change no release output.
- **Expected:** No FP exceptions or uninitialised reads, also in discarded results.
- **Root cause:** (a) the generic RSA z/dz evaluation precedes an if-block whose both branches recompute z and dz; (b) y(2) is not set before the 'compute everything' derivs call; (c) leftover statement before the conditional block that defines hpprime.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** (a) delete equations.f90:3008-3015 (the first z = 1/EFTeomG*... and dz = 1/EFTeomU*... after '! RSA'); (b) 008p4_quintessence.f90:707: add 'y(2) = 0._dl' after 'y(1) = phi_ini'; (c) delete 008p5_Beyond_Horndeski.f90:1171 ('x3ppp = -x3*(hpprime - 3._dl*epsilon_pp) - ...').

### F-095 [S3, unverified] Compiler diagnostics that point at latent bugs in EFTCAMB sources (integer 10**(-1)=0 tolerance, .not. precedence, unset function results, non-interoperable bind(c) dummies, unused/shadowing procedures)

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p7_Extended_Galileon.f90:479` (also: fortran/eftcamb/07f_designer_models/007p5_Omega_Lambda_gamma.f90:811, fortran/eftcamb/06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90:1182, fortran/eftcamb/11_EFTCAMB_python.f90:66, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p2_ADE.f90:612, fortran/eftcamb/02_error_handler.f90:33, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p0_Horndeski.f90:1141)
- **Track:** t1-crash; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** gfortran -Wall -Wextra -Wconversion -O2: (1) 008p7:479 [-Winteger-division] 'do while (sqrt(fsoluction**2) > 10**(-1) .and. counter < 50**1)': 10**(-1) is integer 0, so the bisection tolerance is 0 and the loop always runs 50 iterations; (2) 007p5_Omega_Lambda_gamma.f90:811 '.not. A .and. B .and. C .and. D .and. E' = (.not.A).and.B..., intended .not.(A.and.B...); latent because compute_function_stability is never overridden (always .true.); (3) 06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90:1182/1198/1214 [-Wreturn-type] results of ComputeDeltaTau/DeltaCosmicT/SoundHorizon never set (only MpiStop); (4) 11_EFTCAMB_python.f90:66 [-Wc-binding-type] bind(c) dummies character(len=1) without kind=c_char; (5) unused: 008p2_ADE.f90:612 jacobian, 008p5:1268/1303 BH compute_adotoa/compute_H_derivs (bindings commented out, root of the BH Hubble defect filed elsewhere); (6) [-Wintrinsic-shadow] 02_error_handler.f90:33 'abort', 02p11_stringmod.f90:422 'split'; (7) 007p5:876 / 007p6:884 dummy_solve result unset on the DLSODA failure path; 008p0_Horndeski.f90:1141,1201,1337,1467,1596,1725 num_params_model unset for an unlisted model id (select case without default). Other categories (counts in EFT scope): implicit-interface 1162 (mostly bundled odepack), unused-dummy 428, obsolescent/extension 413, compare-reals 333, unused-variable 231, maybe-uninitialized 90 (equations.f90 s00..sij/alphaM/clxq ones are false positives: set and used under the same evolve_delta_phi/EFT condition), unused-label 8; equations.f90:3804/3806 REAL(8)->REAL(4) is the upstream single-precision transfer array (benign).
- **Expected:** No diagnostics indicating logic errors.
- **Root cause:** Integer arithmetic in a real tolerance, missing parentheses, functions relying on MpiStop to never return, default character kind in bind(c), leftover/commented-out bindings.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** 008p7:479 use 0.1_dl and 50; 007p5:811 'if (.not. (self%OL_Gamma1%compute_function_stability(a) .and. ... .and. self%OL_Omega%compute_function_stability(a))) then'; set the function results (e.g. = 0._dl) before MpiStop in 06_abstract:1170-1214; declare character(kind=c_char) :: key(nkey), value(nvalue); initialise dummy_solve/num_params_model or add case default with an error; remove or wire the unused procedures; rename abort/split.

### F-096 [S3, unverified] DebugEFTCAMB path: positivity-bound dump goes to fort.6666 in the cwd, cache dumps of all OpenMP threads interleave into ~5 GB per model, fixq_array branch evolves no k mode, and stop statements terminate Python

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/03_abstract_EFTCAMB_cache.f90:807` (also: fortran/cmbmain.f90:199-230, fortran/cmbmain.f90:265, fortran/cmbmain.f90:1131, fortran/equations.f90:1975)
- **Track:** t1-crash; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** ADD (-DDEBUG) runs, threads=2: 2_PEFT_Omega_const_1 ok in 807 s and 4_Des_F_R_LCDM_1 ok in 884 s but each wrote 28 files (~5.3 GB, up to 703 MB per file); first 200,000 lines of 2_PEFT_Omega_const_1_cache_FRW.dat contain 38 distinct k with 27,139 k changes between consecutive lines (records interleaved across threads, none torn); a 110 MB 'fort.6666' (821,966 lines, '#a tau k posbound1 ...') appeared in the run directory; 3_RPH_KT_power_1 aborted on the interpolation bounds error (separate finding).
- **Expected:** Debug files under output_root, one coherent stream per k mode (or only for the requested fixq), errors reported through GlobalError.
- **Root cause:** (1) file_Posbounds%unit = 6666 is set and written (lines 807, 869, 1018) but never opened with CreateFile, so gfortran auto-opens fort.6666; (2) the TTextFile units are module-level and open_cache_files is called once on the master EV (cmbmain.f90:226-230) while the k loop runs with PRIVATE(EV) on many threads, and with fixq = 0 every k and every source step is dumped; (3) cmbmain.f90:199-219 resets Evolve_q with Init() and the Ranges_Add calls are commented out, so no k is evolved, then cmbmain.f90:265 'stop'; (4) 'stop' at cmbmain.f90:265, cmbmain.f90:1131 (fixq) and equations.f90:1975 (NaN in cache, inside the OMP region) terminate the interpreter.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Add 'call file_Posbounds%CreateFile(trim(outroot) // 'cache_Posbounds.dat')' next to the other CreateFile calls; dump only when fixq /= 0 (or write per-thread/per-k files, or wrap dump_cache_files in !$OMP CRITICAL); restore the Ranges_Add loop or remove the fixq_array branch; replace the stop statements with GlobalError(...) + return.

### F-097 [S3, unverified] EFTflag/=0 silently forces Accuracy%IntTolBoost>=2; the exact-GR EFT run no longer reproduces EFTflag=0

- **Location:** `fortran/results.f90:398`
- **Track:** t10a-regression; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/t10a-forced-inttolboost-gr-limit.py`
- **Observed:** 2_PEFT_wDE_LCDM_1 (pure EFT, wDE=LCDM, no EFT functions) vs 1_EFT_GR (EFTflag=0) at default settings: tensor TT max rel diff 7.60e-2 (high ell), tensor BB 2.28e-3, lensed TT 2.56e-6, lensed EE 1.65e-6. Against the GR run at IntTolBoost=2 the difference is 4.6e-13 (tensor) and 8.9e-16 (lensed). EFT runs requested with IntTolBoost=1 and with IntTolBoost=2 are bit-identical, so the user value is overridden. The new hook block is absent from eftcamb-pre-rebase.
- **Expected:** An EFT model in the exact GR limit reproduces the EFTflag=0 run at the same user settings (~1e-10). The user's IntTolBoost is honoured, or the change is reported and applied consistently.
- **Root cause:** EFTCAMB hook block at results.f90:394-400: 'if (this%CP%EFTCAMB%EFTflag /= 0) this%CP%Accuracy%IntTolBoost = max(this%CP%Accuracy%IntTolBoost, 2._dl)'. It silently changes the integration and recombination tolerances only for EFT runs. This breaks GR-limit comparisons and EFT-vs-EFTflag=0 response ratios at default settings (the regression audit needed IntTolBoost=2 GR references). Users cannot run EFT models at IntTolBoost<2, and run time grows about 1.3-2x.
- **Proposed fix (physics-changing):** Remove the override. Recommend IntTolBoost=2 for stiff models in the EFTCAMB docs and test inis, or set it as an ini default. Alternatively, apply it only when the user left the default, to the models that need it (Horava/RPH), print it in the EFTCAMB feedback, and apply the same tolerance to EFTflag=0 so GR-limit runs stay comparable.

### F-098 [S3, unverified] derivst EFT block reads local grhog_t/grhor_t before they are assigned

- **Location:** `fortran/equations.f90:4263` (also: fortran/equations.f90:4264, fortran/equations.f90:4341)
- **Track:** t10a-regression; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** In derivst the EFTCAMB block sets EV%eft_cache%gpresdotdotm_t = -(4/3)*Hdot*(grhog_t + grhor_t) + (16/3)*(grhog_t + grhor_t)*adotoa**2 + ... (lines 4263-4264). The locals grhog_t and grhor_t are first assigned at lines 4341-4342. gfortran warns 'grhog_t may be used uninitialized' (equations.f90:4264). Identical in eftcamb-pre-rebase.
- **Expected:** The radiation densities at the current a are used.
- **Root cause:** Hook code copied from derivs uses the derivs locals, but in derivst those locals are only set after the tensor hierarchy block. gpresdotdotm_t, and through compute_H_derivs Hdotdotdot, take stack garbage during tensor evolution. Tensor factors (EFTAT/EFTBT/EFTDT) do not use them, so no spectral effect was identified. NaN/Inf garbage or snan-initialised debug builds can still trap or propagate.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** In lines 4263-4264 replace (grhog_t + grhor_t) with (EV%eft_cache%grhog_t + EV%eft_cache%grhor_t), which are set at lines 4172-4173. Alternatively, move the two assignments 'grhor_t = State%grhornomass/a2; grhog_t = State%grhog/a2' to the top of derivst.

### F-099 [S3, unverified] upstream: get_background(no_thermo=False) segfaults when source windows are set and WantCls=False

- **Location:** `fortran/results.f90:655` (also: fortran/results.f90:665, fortran/results.f90:2153)
- **Track:** t10a-regression; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/t10a-upstream-get-background-windows-segfault.py`
- **Observed:** base_params input (two source windows) with WantCls=False: camb.get_background(cp, no_thermo=False) crashes with SIGSEGV (rc=-11) in A0, B0 (plain CAMB v2) and B1. With windows removed, or WantCls=True, it runs.
- **Expected:** No crash; windows ignored or handled when Cls are not requested.
- **Root cause:** num_redshiftwindows = size(CP%SourceWindows) whenever WantScalars (results.f90:651-656), but Redshift_W is allocated only if WantCls (results.f90:662-665). Thermo initialisation then loops over State%Redshift_W(1:num_redshiftwindows) (results.f90:2153) and touches an unallocated array.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Set num_redshiftwindows = size(SourceWindows) only when WantScalars .and. WantCls (else 0), or allocate Redshift_W whenever num_redshiftwindows > 0.

### F-100 [S3, unverified] upstream: RungeKuttaDP45 with ind=1 resets all settings, so the max_step_size set by GaugeInterface_ScalEv is ignored on the first segment after every evolution switch

- **Location:** `fortran/RungeKuttaDP45.f90:181` (also: fortran/equations.f90:281-285 (caller sets max_step_size 2 Mpc / 20/IntTolBoost), fortran/equations.f90:417-522 (every switch sets ind=1))
- **Track:** t10b-convergence; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** `settings = RungeKuttaDP45Settings(reuse_first_derivative=...)` replaces the caller's max_step_size with the default 20 Mpc. The 2 Mpc limit for q<2e-3 before reionization, and any EFT-specific limit, are therefore lost until the next call with ind=3. With an env-gated variant that keeps max_step_size, GR, Horava_SolSyst_2/3 and eta_4 are bit-identical, so there is no measured impact on the test models. The same line exists in upstream CAMB v2 (B0).
- **Expected:** The caller-supplied max_step_size is honoured on every call (or callers use ind=2 with populated settings).
- **Root cause:** The ind=1 semantics ('reset with default settings') do not match how the caller uses it: it sets max_step_size before calling with ind=1.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** In RungeKuttaDP45 keep max_step_size on ind=1: save it before the structure-constructor reset and restore it after. Alternatively, have GaugeInterface_ScalEv/EvolveTens pass ind=2 after populating rk_settings. Report upstream.

### F-101 [S3, unverified] get_scale_evolution: OpenMP-private EV (and EV%eft_cache) not reset per k, so Beyond-Horndeski evolution depends on thread count / k order

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/11_EFTCAMB_python.f90:316` (also: fortran/cmbmain.f90:250, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p5_Beyond_Horndeski.f90:1009, fortran/equations.f90:2816)
- **Track:** t2-state; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `cd fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/checks/t2-state && python t2a_threads.py run --models 008p5-beyond-horndeski --tag t1 --threads 1 && python t2a_threads.py run --models 008p5-beyond-horndeski --tag t8 --threads 8 && python t2a_fields.py A0 t1 t8 008p5-beyond-horndeski`
- **Observed:** 008p5 Beyond Horndeski (audit/inis/008p5-beyond-horndeski.ini), get_scale_evolution with k=[1e-4,1e-3,1e-2,0.05,0.1,0.3,1], 300 a values:
- OMP 1 vs 2 vs 8: 37 evol fields differ. Worst: evol_delta_DE_eff 3.3e-9, evol_clxb 1.25e-10 and evol_clxc 1.2e-10 relative (k=1e-3 at 2 threads; k=1e-3, 1e-2, 0.05 at 8 threads).
- Same thread count repeated (t1/t1b, t8/t8b/t8c): bit-identical.
- Instrumented tree, EV%eft_cache%initialize() per k: t1, t2 and t8 bit-identical.
- Same tree with the private cache filled with sNaN per k: the whole k=1e-3 evolution is NaN (finite fraction 0.0). A field is therefore read before it is written.
- Cls are unaffected (t1 vs t8 4.6e-15, the GR baseline). All other 40 models are bit-identical apart from the uninitialised fields.
- **Expected:** Each k computed independently of the previous k handled by the same thread; output independent of OMP_NUM_THREADS.
- **Root cause:** EFTCAMB_GetEvolution loops over k with !$OMP PARALLEL DO PRIVATE(EV) and never resets EV%eft_cache (or EV%EFTCAMB_printing/EFTCAMB_QS) between iterations. Each thread's first k sees whatever its private copy held; later k on that thread see the previous k's cache. The order matters because 008p5 BackgroundEFTFunctions builds EFTc, EFTcdot, EFTLambda and EFTLambdadot from eft_cache%adotoa and Hdot (008p5_Beyond_Horndeski.f90:1009-1023). derivs calls it before compute_adotoa (equations.f90:2816-2817), and the model inherits the generic compute_adotoa (06p1:103), which uses EFTc/EFTLambda. adotoa is therefore a fixed-point iteration seeded by stale cache contents. That physics defect is filed separately (repro/beyond-horndeski-hubble.py). This finding is the missing per-k reset that turns it into thread and order dependence. cmbmain.f90:250 has the same PRIVATE(EV) pattern (repro/omp-private-ev-printing-uninit.py).
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** In EFTCAMB_GetEvolution, inside the loop before GetNumEqns:
+            call Ev%eft_cache%initialize()
+            Ev%EFTCAMB_printing = .false.
+            Ev%EFTCAMB_QS = .false.
(and the same in DoSourcek / the cmbmain k loops). Tested (EFT_AUDIT_EVINIT=1 build): t1/t2/t8 bit identical; A0 output changes by <=3.3e-9 relative in 008p5 evolution only. The real fix for BH is to compute adotoa before using it (other finding).

### F-102 [S3, unverified] Horndeski background solver: `integer :: num_eq = 5` is an implicit SAVE, so num_eq=6 leaks to later evolve_hubble=F runs

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p0_Horndeski.f90:2381` (also: also filed by another track as repro/hdsk-num-eq-implicit-save.py)
- **Track:** t2-state; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/hdsk-num-eq-save.py A0`
- **Observed:** On A0, the SAVEd num_eq is read from camblib.so after each run: fresh F run = 5, T run = 6, F run after T run = 6. The F model's outputs (tau0, H, adotoa, OmegaV, EFTc) are bit-identical to a fresh process, so no numerical effect was observed. The F model nonetheless integrates a 6th ODE whose y(6) is unset and whose ydot(6) is never assigned in derivs.
- **Expected:** num_eq = 5 for an evolve_hubble=F, designer_V=F model regardless of history.
- **Root cause:** Initialisation in the declaration gives the local the SAVE attribute. Line 2426 sets it to 6 when evolve_hubble or designerV is true, and nothing resets it to 5.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** -    integer :: num_eq = 5
+    integer :: num_eq
 ...
+    num_eq = 5
     if (self%evolve_hubble .or. self%designerV) then
         num_eq = 6
     end if
Tested in work/t2-state/fix: repro reports 'no leak'; outputs bit identical.

### F-103 [S3, confirmed] QS_mu, QS_sigma, QS_mu_gen, QS_sigma_gen are 0/0 = NaN (no guard) for shipped models whose Omega', c, Gamma2, Gamma5 vanish

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90:920` (also: fortran/eftcamb/06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90:972 (QS_sigma), fortran/eftcamb/06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90:1012-1062 (QS_mu_gen/QS_sigma_gen), fortran/equations.f90:3218-3220 (mu, gamma, sigma_eft))
- **Track:** t2d-static; reported severity S2, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/qs-mu-nan-vanishing-denominator.py`
- **Observed:** A0, k=0.01,0.1 /Mpc, a=0.05,0.5,1 (re-run in attempt 4, identical). 2_PEFT_gamma1_const_1: QS_mu, QS_sigma, QS_mu_gen, QS_sigma_gen NaN at 6/6 points while the code's own mu = 1. 2_PEFT_gamma4_const_1: QS_mu and QS_sigma NaN 6/6, mu = 0.9999. Scan of 72 shipped models (16 points each): all 16 points NaN in 7 models (2_PEFT_gamma1_const_1/2, gamma4_const_1, gamma6_const_1, gamma6_exp_1, gamma6_lin_1, gamma6_power_1). 5_ADE_1 has mu/gamma/sigma_eft non-finite at 1/16 points.
- **Expected:** Finite values; these models have a GR-like quasi-static limit (mu=1).
- **Root cause:** The closed-form QS ratios (06_abstract:908-1062) have numerator and denominator that are both sums of terms proportional to EFTOmegaP, EFTc, EFTGamma2V, EFTGamma5V; when all vanish the code evaluates 0/0 without a guard. The equations.f90:3218-3220 mu/gamma/sigma_eft outputs likewise divide by dgrho+3dgq*adotoa/k(+2dgpi) without a guard. In debug mode EV%eft_cache%is_nan then stops the run (cmbmain.f90:614-617).
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Compute numerator and denominator separately. If |denominator| <= tiny*(sum of |terms|), return the GR/k->infinity limit (1) or fall back to the general QS formula, and mark the field. Same guard for mu/gamma/sigma_eft in equations.f90.
- **Verifier (reproduce):** CONFIRMED, severity S3; The defect is real: the closed-form QS_mu/QS_sigma (06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90:920 and 972) and QS_mu_gen/QS_sigma_gen (1054-1059) evaluate 0/0 with no guard. This happens whenever OmegaP, c, Gamma2 and Gamma5 vanish, and for the _gen pair also Gamma3, Gamma4 and Gamma6. I reproduced it with the given script and with an independent route: the harness get_scale_evolution on a dense 300-point a-grid at 4 k values, plus a symbolic proof. The defect is in EFTCAMB code, not upstream CAMB and not the test.

I lower the severity to S3, for three reasons:
- These four fields are diagnostics only. They are computed at equations.f90:3766-3768 and written to the cache, dumps and the Python getters. Nothing in the evolution reads them, and the code's own mu/gamma/sigma_eft for the same models are exactly 1. Cls and P(k) are unaffected.
- For these degenerate models the quasi-static ratio is formally undefined, because the field has no gradient coupling to the metric. NaN is a poor output, not wrong physics.
- The run stops only in a -DDEBUG build.

Corrections to the finding:
- The equations.f90 lines are 3783-3785, not 3218-3220.
- The debug NaN stop is at equations.f90:1972-1976 (in subroutine output), not cmbmain.f90:614-617.
- I did not reproduce the side claim that mu/gamma/sigma_eft are non-finite for 5_ADE_1: I found 0/1200 non-finite on my grid. If it happens at all, it is an isolated point where the denominator crosses zero.
- The source of QS_sigma is a verbatim copy of QS_mu. This overlaps the known Sigma-copy fact.

### F-104 [S3, confirmed] RPH parameter getters size the PlanckMass_ODE block with RPH_Tensor_ODE%parameter_number

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/07f_designer_models/007p2_RPH.f90:610` (also: fortran/eftcamb/07f_designer_models/007p2_RPH.f90:731, fortran/eftcamb/07f_designer_models/007p2_RPH.f90:852)
- **Track:** t2d-static; reported severity S2, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/rph-param-index-tensor-ode.py [/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-marco-IW-eftcamb-update-EFTCAMB/2b9dddd0-ab26-46fb-8810-571fb7648a45/scratchpad/audit/work/t2d-static/FIX2]`
- **Observed:** 3_RPH_MK_power_1 + RPHmassPmodel_ODE=1 (RPHmassP_ODE0=0.123), Tensor_ODE off (re-run in attempt 4, identical). A0: num_params 3, names ['RPHmassP0','RPHkineticity0',''], values [0.1, 0.1, 0.0]. FIX2 (index fixed): names [...,'RPHmassP_ODE0'], values [0.1, 0.1, 0.123].
- **Expected:** The last parameter is RPHmassP_ODE0 = 0.123.
- **Root cause:** In ParameterNames (l.610), ParameterNamesLatex (l.731) and ParameterValues (l.852), `NMo = NT + self%RPH_Tensor_ODE%parameter_number` when RPHusealphaM=F. ComputeParametersNumber (l.515) and InitModelParameters (l.413) use RPH_PlanckMass_ODE, so whenever the two counts differ every later parameter is mapped to the wrong block or to none (intent(out) left unset).
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Replace RPH_Tensor_ODE with RPH_PlanckMass_ODE on the three NMo lines (FIX2, fix2.diff).
- **Verifier (reproduce):** CONFIRMED, severity S3; The defect is real and the root cause is correct. In 007p2_RPH.f90 at lines 610, 731 and 852 (A0 tree, commit f6f4ba9 = 5bef49a), the RPHusealphaM=F branch sets NMo = NT + RPH_Tensor_ODE%parameter_number. ComputeParametersNumber (l.515) and InitModelParameters (l.413) use RPH_PlanckMass_ODE instead. Whenever the PlanckMass_ODE and Tensor_ODE parameter counts differ, every parameter after NT is mapped to the wrong block or to none. The repro shows this, and so does an independent multi-case check of names, latex and values on A0 and AD. When the counts are equal (both ODE blocks on) the output is correct, which is the predicted signature. The same lines exist in pre-rebase EFTCAMB (B1 l.572/693/814), so this is a long-standing EFTCAMB bug, not a rebase regression and not upstream CAMB.

I lowered the severity from S2 to S3. ParameterNames, ParameterNamesLatex and ParameterValues are called only from the Python bridge (11_EFTCAMB_python.f90:214/228/242 -> camb/eftcamb.py param_names/param_values). They are never used in the physics, and InitModelParameters, the setter, is correct. So Cls, P(k) and the background are unaffected; only the reported metadata (names, labels, values) is wrong, and only for RPH with mass-ODE or tensor-ODE blocks of unequal size. The proposed fix is right and safe-local: replace RPH_Tensor_ODE with RPH_PlanckMass_ODE on the three NMo lines. One caveat: the original FIX2 tree no longer exists after the scratch wipe, so I did not rebuild it to re-measure the after-fix numbers.

### F-105 [S3, unverified] Implicit SAVE: Horndeski `integer :: num_eq = 5` keeps 6 after an evolve_hubble/designer_V run; later runs integrate an undefined 6th component

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p0_Horndeski.f90:2381` (also: fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p0_Horndeski.f90:2420)
- **Track:** t2d-static; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/hdsk-num-eq-implicit-save.py`
- **Observed:** num_eq is 5 in a fresh process and 6 after a Horndeski_evolve_hubble=T run (track 2 repro hdsk-num-eq-save.py). For 5_hdsk_TG_1 run F, then T, then F in one process, outputs are bit-identical to the fresh run (H(z), adotoa, Hdot, OmegaV, EFTc, tau0 max rel diff 0; re-run in attempt 4), so no output effect was measured. y(6) is allocated but never set, and ydot(6) (intent(out)) is never assigned for evolve_hubble=F, so behaviour depends on memory contents.
- **Expected:** num_eq = 5 for every run with evolve_hubble=F and designer_V=F.
- **Root cause:** Initialisation in a declaration gives the local the SAVE attribute; line 2426 only ever raises it to 6. The other initialised local (l.2420 integration_error_message='') is harmless. These are the only 2 initialised locals in EFTCAMB procedures.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Declare `integer :: num_eq` and `character(len=1024) :: integration_error_message`, then assign `num_eq = 5; integration_error_message = ''` as executable statements at the top of the routine.

### F-106 [S3, unverified] Kind-less (single precision) real literals in double-precision EFT expressions; one changes shipped results above 1e-8

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p6_Scaling_Cubic.f90:247` (also: fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p0_Horndeski.f90:2767, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p0_Horndeski.f90:3330, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p7_Extended_Galileon.f90:574, fortran/eftcamb/10_EFTCAMB_background_output.f90:52,54, fortran/cmbmain.f90:640 (0.7, inherited from upstream), per-file counts: work/t2d-static/real_literals.txt)
- **Track:** t2d-static; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** 110 kind-less literals in EFTCAMB + MOD code, 7 not exactly representable in single precision. Patched tree (work/t2d-static/lit.diff) vs A0 on 5_scg: worst Cl 1.53e-7, worst P(k) 3.77e-7, bg_H 3.4e-8, sigma8 3.9e-9. Only `self%SCG_y20 = 4.74*1.d-9` (initial bisection guess) matters for shipped inis, which also shows that the SCG shooting result depends on its starting point at the 1e-7 level. The other inexact ones are in branches no shipped ini exercises.
- **Expected:** Literals with _dl; results independent of the initial guess at below 1e-8.
- **Root cause:** 4.74, 0.99, 0.01, 0.02, 1.e-5, 0.01 written without kind are converted from single precision (rel. error 1e-8 to 5e-8).
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Use 4.74e-9_dl (008p6:247), 0.99_dl (008p0:2767), 0.01_dl (008p0:3330), 0.02_dl/.5_dl (008p7:574), 1.e-5_dl/0.01_dl (10_EFTCAMB_background_output.f90:52,54); also tighten the SCG/BH shooting tolerance (abs(tol) > 1.d-6).

### F-107 [S3, unverified] Undefined function results / intent(out) on early returns, uninitialised locals, assignments to absent optional dummies (grouped hygiene)

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p5_Beyond_Horndeski.f90:1171` (also: fortran/eftcamb/07f_designer_models/007p8_3p1_wDE_designer.f90:726,734, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p0_Horndeski.f90:1141,1201,1337,1467,1596,1725, fortran/eftcamb/06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90:1182,1198,1214, fortran/eftcamb/02_root_finding.f90:120, fortran/eftcamb/07f_designer_models/007p5_Omega_Lambda_gamma.f90:876, fortran/eftcamb/07f_designer_models/007p6_Omega_Lambda_alpha.f90:884, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p5_Beyond_Horndeski.f90:262-293,497, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p6_Scaling_Cubic.f90:257-290,499, fortran/eftcamb/05_abstract_parametrizations_2D.f90:374, fortran/eftcamb/07f_designer_models/007p3_Designer_fR.f90:337,781, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p0_Horndeski.f90:2423, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p4_quintessence.f90:697, fortran/equations.f90:196, fortran/equations.f90:4264 (derivst grhog_t/grhor_t uninitialised: track 1 repro derivst-tensor-uninit-grhog.py))
- **Track:** t2d-static; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** gfortran warnings and static scans. (a) 008p5:1171 x3ppp built from hpprime before it is set (-Wuninitialized); the result is unused, but it traps under -finit-real=snan -ffpe-trap=invalid. (b) 007p8:726/734 N3/N3o read uninitialised in `.not. LuminalGW .and. i <= N3` (.and. does not short-circuit). (c) 008p0 num_params_model: select case without a default (l.1125-1139 and five more). (d) Base stubs EFTCAMBModelComputeDeltaTau/DeltaCosmicT/SoundHorizon (06_abstract:1182/1198/1214) return no value (-Wreturn-type). (e) zbrent (02_root_finding:120) and OL dummy_solve (007p5:876, 007p6:884) return an undefined value on failure. In OL, zbrent's `succes` and dummy_solve's host-associated `success` are the same variable (aliasing). (f) BH/SCG shooting (008p5:262-293, 008p6:257-290): unbounded `do while (abs(tol) > 1.d-6)`, success flag ignored, intent(out) tol undefined after a failed DLSODA, MpiStop on bracket failure. (g) `if (.not. present(x)) x = ...` writes to an absent optional at 05_abstract_parametrizations_2D.f90:374, 007p3:337, 008p0:2423, 008p4:697; no current caller omits the argument. (h) EvolutionVars%EFTCAMB_QS (equations.f90:196) is never read.
- **Expected:** No reads of undefined values, all results defined on every path, loops bounded, no writes to absent optionals.
- **Root cause:** Missing initialisation/defaults and early returns without setting outputs.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** (a) delete the dead x3ppp line at 1171; (b) nest the LuminalGW test; (c) add `case default; num_params_model = 0` (or error); (d) set the result to 0 before MpiStop/error; (e) set the result (e.g. huge or NaN) before return and avoid modifying zbrent's flag via host association; (f) add an iteration cap and check success; (g) use a local copy (`logical :: flag; flag = .true.; if (present(x)) flag = x`); (h) remove EFTCAMB_QS.

### F-108 [S3, unverified] Exact real ==, /= comparisons select physics branches or guard divisions (grouped)

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_IC.f90:257` (also: fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_IC.f90:295, fortran/equations.f90:3237-3238, fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_stability.f90:302-303,339-340, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p5_Beyond_Horndeski.f90:1005,1027,1187,1367, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p7_Extended_Galileon.f90:182,337,519,522, fortran/eftcamb/06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90:1159, fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_RGR.f90:74)
- **Track:** t2d-static; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** 71 -Wcompare-reals in EFTCAMB + MOD code. With physical meaning: IC type chosen by Gamma1..6 /= 0 (09_EFTCAMB_IC.f90:257-259); division guards `EFTpiC + k2*EFTpiD1 + k2**2*EFTpiD2 /= 0` (09_EFTCAMB_IC.f90:295) and `(EFTpiA1 + k2*EFTpiA2) == 0`, `(EFTpiB1 + k2*EFTpiB2) == 0` (equations.f90:3237-3238), which only catch exact zeros; stability-formula selection by `Gamma6V /= 0 .or. (Gamma3V+Gamma4V) /= 0` (09_EFTCAMB_stability.f90:302-303, 339-340); BH `x4 == 0` branches (008p5:1005,1027,1187,1367); ExtGal `adotoa == 0` re-computation triggers (008p7:337,519,522); 06_abstract:1159 `D1 /= 0`. No wrong result was measured with shipped inis, where these quantities are exactly 0 or clearly non-zero. (The ExtGal adotoa==0 trigger is also why its background EFT functions use a stale background in derivs: see fullmap-bg-eft-stale-background.)
- **Expected:** Tolerance-based tests (relative to the scale of the terms) where round-off can change the branch, and guards that catch near-zero denominators.
- **Root cause:** Floating-point equality used as a model-type test and as a division guard.
- **Proposed fix (unknown):** Replace with explicit model flags (e.g. the model already knows whether Gamma_i are active) or |x| <= eps*scale tests; guard near-zero pi-equation denominators.

### F-109 [S3, unverified] Latent thread-safety hygiene: lazy allocation of shared spline5 yint from per-k code; debug cache dumps to shared file units from inside the OpenMP k loop

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/04f_parametrizations_1D/04p019_spline5_parametrizations_1D.f90:647` (also: fortran/cmbmain.f90:611-623, fortran/eftcamb/03_abstract_EFTCAMB_cache.f90:50-64, fortran/results.f90:819 (parallel DeltaTimeArr calling dtauda -> EFT compute_* routines))
- **Track:** t2d-static; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** SplineQ1DIntegral allocates and fills self%yint on first use (l.647-648, 697-708). It is reachable from compute_adotoa (designer f(R), ShiftSym), which runs inside OpenMP loops (cmbmain k loop, results.f90:819 DeltaTimeArr). In every shipped flow the first call happens serially during model initialisation, and no shipped ini uses spline5 (EFTwDE=10). The 16-model 1-vs-6-thread test was bit-identical apart from lensing. With DebugEFTCAMB, cmbmain.f90:611-623 calls EV%eft_cache%dump_cache_files() and `stop` from inside the parallel k loop, so all threads write the module-level TTextFile units of 03_abstract_EFTCAMB_cache.f90:50-64 concurrently.
- **Expected:** No writes to shared objects reachable from parallel regions.
- **Root cause:** Lazy initialisation in a type-bound function; debug I/O placed inside the parallel region.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Allocate/compute yint in SplineQ1DInitParams (or protect it with !$OMP CRITICAL); in debug mode dump under !$OMP CRITICAL or per-thread files, and replace `stop` by an error flag.

### F-110 [S3, unverified] Exact GR/LCDM points rejected by stability checks on roundoff-level quantities

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_stability.f90:339`
- **Track:** t3-gr; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/checks/t3-gr/b11_more_families.py`
- **Observed:** 3+1 designer GR point (with the R7 fix): 'beyond GLPV' rejection, because EFTGamma3V+EFTGamma4V = 1/3 - 1 + 2/3 = -1.1e-16. Horndeski model-7 LCDM point (no shooting): ghost kinetic term -5.4e-21. Beyond Horndeski want_LCDM: gradient term -1.4e-25 at a=3e-3. Omega_Lambda_gamma GR point (with the R5 fix): ghost kinetic -9.4e-3, from the background solver's c ~ -2.6e-4 residue at a=1e-9 (relative 1e-11).
- **Expected:** Points that are GR by construction are accepted (no propagating scalar).
- **Root cause:** The ghost, gradient and GLPV tests compare quantities that vanish identically at the GR point with exact zero (EFTCAMB_stability_threshold=0 in the test inis) and no relative tolerance. The `/= 0._dl` test for Gamma6, Gamma3+Gamma4 has no tolerance either.
- **Proposed fix (physics-changing):** Use relative tolerances (e.g. |Gamma3+Gamma4| > 1e-12*(|Gamma3|+|Gamma4|+1)), and skip the scalar stability tests when the kinetic term is below a tolerance relative to its GR-normalised scale.

### F-111 [S3, unverified] EFTCAMB changes reionization max_redshift 50 -> 1000 for every run, changing EFTflag=0 results vs CAMB by 2.4e-5 (undocumented)

- **Location:** `fortran/reionization.f90:48`

- **Expected:** EFTflag=0 equal to CAMB, or the change documented for users.
- **Root cause:** The MOD block 'larger redshift range for the reionization search' changes the default bracket of the tau -> z_re root search. This shifts z_re within the solver tolerance for all models; it is not tied to EFTflag and not in any user docs.
- **Proposed fix (physics-changing):** Document it (README/docs and the Python ReionizationModel.max_redshift docstring), or keep 50 and enlarge the bracket only when the search fails or EFTflag/=0.

### F-116 [S3, unverified] Fourier min_a0=T with both orders 0 gives value a0 - 1e16

### F-117 [S3, unverified] Quintic spline with Logx/Logy reports log-transformed parameters; garbled feedback formats

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/04f_parametrizations_1D/04p019_spline5_parametrizations_1D.f90:262` (also: fortran/eftcamb/04f_parametrizations_1D/04p019_spline5_parametrizations_1D.f90:295-296,138-147,541,597)
- **Track:** t4a-param; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** Logx_Flag=T, node x1=1e-3: parameter_value(1) = -6.9078 and feedback prints 'EFTwx1 = -6.907755'. init_parameters(parameter_value(:)) round trip aborts with 'spline x coordinate must be non-negative' (2 of 60 driver cases). Feedback lines 'whether interpolate in terms of log(x):' print raw bytes (logical written with '(a,a)'). Error messages use '(a,a,a,I3)' with 2 strings, and say 'non-negative' for a > 0 test.
- **Expected:** parameter_value returns the user-facing values that init_parameters accepts; readable feedback.
- **Root cause:** SplineQ1DParameterValues returns self%x_coord / self%pixels after the in-place log transform (lines 135-149). Format descriptors mismatch their items (lines 295-296, 541, 597).
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Return exp(x_coord(i)) / exp(pixels(i)) when the flags are set (or keep the raw arrays). Use '(a,L2)' for the flags and '(a,a,I3)' for the order message; say 'positive'.

### F-118 [S3, unverified] 2D abstract feedback assigns to an absent optional argument (segfault)

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/05_abstract_parametrizations_2D.f90:374`
- **Track:** t4a-param; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** zero_parametrization_2D%feedback() without argument: SIGSEGV (rc=-11). With the fix: rc=0. constant/bilinear override feedback and are fine. No model currently instantiates 2D parametrizations, so this is latent.
- **Expected:** Optional print_params defaults to .true. without writing to it.
- **Root cause:** `if (.not. present(print_params)) print_params = .true.` writes to a non-present dummy.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Use a local print_params_temp = .true.; if (present(print_params)) print_params_temp = print_params (as the 1D abstract class does; patch 'feedback2d', tested).

### F-119 [S3, unverified] Latent wrong higher derivatives: axion d4=0, step d4 off by delta^-9 (dead type), exponential d3/d4 cancellation at a<1e-6

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/04f_parametrizations_1D/04p015_axion_potential.f90:243` (also: fortran/eftcamb/04f_parametrizations_1D/04p017_step_parametrizations_1D.f90:322, fortran/eftcamb/04f_parametrizations_1D/04p004_exponential_parametrizations_1D.f90)
- **Track:** t4a-param; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** Axion1DFourthDerivative returns 0 (exact e.g. -375.5 at phi=0.9). stepParametrized1DFourthDerivative relative error 1.0e9 (v1=-1, v2=-0.8, aT=0.3, delta=0.1), 0 after the fix. Exponential (flag 4) with exponent 1 or 2 at a=1e-8: d4 code 0 vs exact 3.0, d3 code 0 vs 1e-3; the a^k-weighted error is <= 3e-15.
- **Expected:** Correct derivatives, or no deferred binding claiming them.
- **Root cause:** Axion d4 is unimplemented (returns 0). Step d4 has a wrong prefactor 1/(delta^7 (...)^4.5) instead of delta^2/(...)^4.5 (04p017:322-325). Exponential d3/d4 are written as x^(n-4)*(polynomial) that cancels catastrophically for small x.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Implement the axion d4 (or MpiStop with a clear message). Fix the step prefactor (tested). Optionally rewrite the exponential derivatives in un-factored form. No current consumer is affected: quintessence uses only V, V', V''; step_parametrization_1D is never allocated; EFT functions enter as a^k d^k f.

### F-120 [S3, unverified] upstream: CAMB v2 massive-neutrino rho/p fits and drho violate d rho/dtau = -3H(rho+p) at ~1e-4 of rho_nu (propagates into EFT Hdot at 3e-6)

- **Location:** `fortran/massive_neutrinos.f90:341` (also: fortran/massive_neutrinos.f90:185 (rho_P fits), fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_IC.f90:143,174,442,564, fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_RGR.f90:256,286, fortran/eftcamb/06p1_abstract_EFTCAMB_full_map.f90:276,305, fortran/eftcamb/06p2_abstract_EFTCAMB_designer.f90:161,189)
- **Track:** t4b-derivs; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** From the A0 cache (a^2-weighted, relative to adotoa*grhonu_tot): grhonudot_tot (ThermalNuBackground%drho) vs FD of grhonu_tot (rho_P fit) 9.8e-5; FD(grhonu_tot) vs -3H(grhonu_tot+gpinu_tot) 8.5e-5; drho vs -3H(rho+p) 9.1e-5 (FD spread 1.7e-6). In the active range grhonudot_tot fails in 58/62 models at 5e-6..1.9e-5. Full-mapping Hdot (quintessence x5) and total-matter conservation fail at 3.0e-6 and 4.9e-6; they pass exactly with omnuh2=0, and B1 (CAMB 1.6.5 tables) gives 3.4e-8 for quint Hdot. gpinudot and gpinudotdot, from EFTCAMB's exact derivatives of the p fit, are consistent to 1e-10.
- **Expected:** Background massive-neutrino density, pressure and density derivative mutually consistent to ~1e-7.
- **Root cause:** rho, p and drho in CAMB v2 are separate rational fits, each accurate to ~1e-4 in derivative relations. EFTCAMB uses drho for grhonudot_tot and combines rho and p in its Hdot formulas.
- **Proposed fix (physics-changing):** Upstream: derive p_nu from the rho fit (p = -rho - (1/3) d rho/d ln a) or fit jointly. EFTCAMB side: compute grhonudot_tot = -3*adotoa*(grhonu_tot + gpinu_tot) (09_EFTCAMB_IC, RGR, stability, 06p1/06p2, 11_python, equations.f90) instead of ThermalNuBackground%drho, so that at least EFTCAMB is internally consistent.

### F-121 [S3, unverified] MassiveNuVars dgp uses delta p = (p/rho) delta rho for massive neutrinos instead of the available exact pressure perturbation

- **Location:** `fortran/equations.f90:1541`
- **Track:** t4b-derivs; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** dgp = dgp + gpnu_t*clxnu (EFTCAMB MOD) feeds dgpnu into dz (equations.f90:3137), the pi/Einstein factors (3049, 3181) and dp_matter. A candidate patch using the exact delta p (Nu_Integrate_L012 for the integrated case, y(nu_ix+1) for the approximate hierarchy; diff work/t4b-derivs/fix_diffs/massive-nu-dgp.diff) changes spectra by at most 1.3e-7: 4_Des_F_R_LCDM_1 PP 1.27e-7, pk 4.6e-7, sigma8 3.7e-8; 2_PEFT_Omega_const_1 8e-10; 3_RPH_KT_power_1 4.7e-9; 4_Des_mc5e_wCDM_1 5.1e-9. It does not remove the ~5e-6 dz/sigmadot finite-difference residuals.
- **Expected:** Massive-neutrino pressure perturbation consistent with CAMB's momentum integrals.
- **Root cause:** Approximation introduced in the EFTCAMB modification of MassiveNuVars (dgp optional argument).
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Use the exact delta p from Nu_Integrate_L012 (or the evolved delta p of the approximate hierarchy) as in the candidate diff, or document the approximation.

### F-122 [S3, unverified] RGR feedback compares the RGR time with itself: 'coincide' warning always printed, and no warning when perturbations are never switched on

- **Location:** `fortran/results.f90:604` (also: fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_RGR.f90:73-76)

With threshold 1e-7, mc5e/quint print 'Return to GR time: 0.1100E+01' and the same 'far from GR at initial time' warning, although the perturbations are in fact never switched on.
- **Expected:** Warn only when RGR_time equals the requested turn-on, and warn explicitly when RGR_time > 1 (EFT perturbations disabled)
- **Root cause:** results.f90:604 overwrites EFTCAMB_pert_turn_on with RGR_time before calling EFTCAMBReturnToGR_feedback with EFTCAMB_pert_turn_on as initial_time, so the test at 09_EFTCAMB_RGR.f90:73 is always true.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Save the requested turn-on before line 604 and pass it as initial_time; add a warning when RGR_time > 1.

- **Location:** `camb/camb.py:274` (also: fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_main.f90:505, fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_stability.f90:400-417)

### F-126 [S3, unverified] Fixed 3x1000-point sampler (no refinement) misses instabilities narrower than ~1e-3 in a at intermediate times, even with the stale-y fix and ghost_math on

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_stability.f90:66`
- **Track:** t6-stability; reported severity S3, confidence high

- **Observed:** Ghost window |a-0.2998| < 2e-4 (39 unstable fine-grid points, EFT_kinetic(a0) = -3.53e-10). No sampler point falls inside. Verdict stable on A0 and on fixy, with ghost_math F and T (A1 changes sign twice between samples).
- **Expected:** Short-lived ghost or gradient instabilities are detected, or the resolution is documented.
- **Root cause:** The linear pass has step ~1e-3. The log passes are dense only near astart and aend. Sign-change detection compares consecutive samples only.
- **Proposed fix (physics-changing):** Add refinement: bisect around local minima or sign changes of the normalised coefficients (EFT_kinetic, EFT_gradient, AT, DT, A1) between samples, or use >= N points per e-fold. At least document the resolution.

### F-128 [S3, unverified] Stability reporting and coverage hygiene: no reason in the exception, beyond-GLPV warning ignores feedback level, hard-coded k_max=10, shipped flags accept f(R) B0<0

### F-129 [S3, unverified] Uninitialised locals read in EFT blocks: w_dark_energy_t (derivs) and grhog_t/grhor_t (derivst)

- **Location:** `fortran/equations.f90:3692` (also: fortran/equations.f90:4263-4264, fortran/equations.f90:2753-2755, fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/derivst-tensor-uninit-grhog.py (t1))
- **Track:** t8a-equations; reported severity S3, confidence high

- **Observed:** Build PS (-O0 -finit-real=snan) with FP traps on: scalar-only 2_PEFT_Omega_const_1 traps (signal 4), and runs clean when w_dark_energy_t=-1 is set for EFT. Tensor-only run traps, and runs clean when grhog_t/grhor_t are assigned before the EFT tensor block. Release build: tensor Cls change 0.0 with the fix (5_Horava_eta_4, 5_quint_exponential).

It is then read in gpres = gpres_noDE + w_dark_energy_t*grhov_t (3692), where 0*NaN gives NaN, and gpres goes to phidot and the window sources. In derivst, gpresdotdotm_t (4263-4264) uses grhog_t/grhor_t, which are only assigned at 4341-4342. Both pre-existing; the derivst one is also filed by t1.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** In 2753-2754 add w_dark_energy_t = -1._dl in the EFT branch (or guard 3692 with EFTflag==0). In derivst use EV%eft_cache%grhog_t/grhor_t at 4263-4264, or move the grhog_t/grhor_t assignments before the EFT block.

### F-131 [S3, unverified] EFT massive-neutrino pressure perturbation approximated as p_nu*delta_nu instead of the exact momentum integral

- **Location:** `fortran/equations.f90:1540` (also: fortran/equations.f90:1523-1530)
- **Track:** t8a-equations; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/checks/t8a-equations/targeted_tests.py dgp`
- **Observed:** Exact dp_nu (Nu_Integrate_L012) vs dgp = gpnu_t*clxnu with omnuh2=0.01: worst_cl 2.0e-5 (5_hdsk_TG_1), 1.07e-4 (5_hdsk_TG_3), sigma8 2.4e-5.
- **Expected:** delta p_nu from the distribution function (equals w*delta_rho only in the relativistic and non-relativistic limits).
- **Root cause:** MassiveNuVars 'compatibility with massive neutrinos' block uses the adiabatic approximation. dgpnu feeds EFT dz (3049, 3181), the delta_phi dp_matter (3153) and EFTpiE.
- **Proposed fix (physics-changing):** In the non-approximated branch call Nu_Integrate_L012 (returns dpnu) and use dgp = dgp + grhormass_t*dpnu. In the MassiveNuApprox branch use y(nu_ix+1)*rhonu.

### F-132 [S3, confirmed] set_classes(dark_energy_model!='EFTCAMB') / set_dark_energy() on params with an active EFT model are silently ignored (EFTflag stays on)

- **Location:** `camb/model.py:873` (also: camb/model.py:892 (set_dark_energy), camb/model.py:923 (set_dark_energy_w_a))
- **Track:** t8b-integration; reported severity S2, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/set-dark-energy-ignored-with-active-eft.py`
- **Observed:** 2_PEFT_wDE_wCDM_1 (EFTflag=1): H(z=1) = 129.6477. After set_dark_energy(w=-0.7, 'ppf'), or set_classes('ppf') + DarkEnergy.set_params(w=-0.7): EFTflag=1, H(z=1) = 129.6477. Plain CAMB PPF w=-0.7: 131.9315 (-1.73e-2 difference). No warning.
- **Expected:** As the EFTCAMB MOD comment states ('When dark_energy_model \= EFTCAMB EFTCAMB is initialized with EFTflag = 0'), selecting another dark-energy model disables EFTCAMB, or an error is raised.
- **Root cause:** The elif branch of the set_classes EFTCAMB MOD only replaces self.DarkEnergy. set_dark_energy / set_dark_energy_w_a do not touch EFTCAMB either. With EFTflag/=0, dtauda and the perturbations use the EFT model and never read CP%DarkEnergy.
- **Proposed fix (unknown):** In set_classes, elif dark_energy_model: also do self.EFTCAMB = self.make_class_named('EFTCAMB', EFTCAMB) (fresh object, EFTflag=0). In set_dark_energy and set_dark_energy_w_a, reset EFTCAMB the same way, or raise CAMBError if self.EFTCAMB.EFTflag != 0.
- **Verifier (reproduce):** CONFIRMED, severity S3; I reproduced the effect exactly. I also confirmed it independently through a different path: EFTCAMB initialised from a dict instead of read_ini, the change made with set_dark_energy_w_a instead of set_dark_energy, and CMB TT compared instead of only H. The root cause is right and lies in the code under audit, not upstream CAMB. In camb/model.py, the elif branch of the set_classes EFTCAMB MOD (around line 873) only replaces self.DarkEnergy. set_dark_energy (line 892) and set_dark_energy_w_a (line 923) never touch self.EFTCAMB. When EFTflag != 0, dtauda (fortran/equations.f90:23-40) and the perturbation equations ignore CP%DarkEnergy entirely. The MOD comment ("When dark_energy_model \= EFTCAMB EFTCAMB is initialized with EFTflag = 0") describes behaviour the code does not have. The odd part is that set_classes('EFTCAMB') is the call that resets EFTflag to 0, which is the reverse of what a user would expect. I lower the severity from S2 to S3. The physics computed is correct for the EFT model that is actually configured, and EFTCAMB owns the dark-energy sector by design. The defect is that an API call silently has no effect, with no warning, and the comment is misleading. It is not wrong physics in a supported model. On the proposed fix: resetting EFTCAMB in the set_classes elif branch is compatible with camb.set_params/read_ini, because EFT_do_set(initialize_parameters) runs after do_set(set_classes). It does change the API, since dark_energy_model='fluid'/'ppf' would then wipe an EFT setup. Raising CAMBError or logging a warning when EFTflag != 0 in set_dark_energy and set_dark_energy_w_a is the less surprising option. I class the fix as an API/behaviour change, not physics-changing for the configured model.

### F-133 [S3, unverified] EFTCAMB changes TTanhReionization max_redshift default 50 -> 1000 for all runs (GR differs from CAMB by 2.3e-5; z_re range guard disabled)

- **Location:** `fortran/reionization.f90:48`
- **Track:** t8b-integration; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/checks/t8b-integration/reion_maxz.py`
- **Observed:** EFTflag=0, same ini: A0 vs B0 max rel diff EE 2.29e-5, BB 1.60e-5, TE 2.4e-6, TT 1.1e-6; z_re = 10.958642 (A0) vs 10.958691 (B0). A0 with Reion.max_redshift=50 vs B0: 1.1e-14 (EE), 1.4e-13 (BB). B0 with 1000 vs A0: 1.0e-14.
- **Expected:** GR results equal to CAMB v2 (default 50); out-of-range reionization (z_re + 3 delta_z > max) still reported.
- **Root cause:** The tau -> z_re bisection starts from [min_redshift, max_redshift] and stops at a bracket width of 1e-2/boost, followed by linear interpolation. A different starting bracket ends at a slightly different z_re (5e-5), which moves the low-l EE/BB. With 1000 the 'Reionization redshift out of range' guard only triggers for z_re close to recombination. The value comes from the pre-rebase version; in v2 it applies to every model and is only settable from Python (Reion.max_redshift / max_zrei), not from the Fortran ini.
- **Proposed fix (physics-changing):** Restore max_redshift = 50._dl (users needing more can set max_zrei / Reion.max_redshift), or set 1000 only when EFTflag /= 0 and read max_zrei in TTanhReionization ReadParams for ini users.

### F-134 [S3, unverified] Lensing sanity check turned into a silent skip: unrealistic lensing amplitude returns all-zero lensed Cls with no error (also in GR)

- **Location:** `fortran/lensing.f90:306` (also: fortran/lensing.f90:328, fortran/lensing.f90:448, fortran/lensing.f90:785, fortran/lensing.f90:1016, fortran/lensing.f90:1194, fortran/lensing.f90:205 (AmplitudeError now unused))
- **Track:** t8b-integration; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/checks/t8b-integration/lens_amplitude.py A0 B0`
- **Observed:** GR, As=2.1e-9, Alens=300 (Cphil3(10) > 1e-7): A0 returns lensed_scalar Cls identically 0.0 (TT_200 = 0, BB max = 0) with no error. Upstream B0 raises CAMBError 'You need to normalize realistically to use lensing'.
- **Expected:** A catchable CAMBError (AmplitudeError is already a GlobalError, not a stop, in CAMB 1.6.5 and v2), or at least an error/warning flag. Not zeros that look like valid output.
- **Root cause:** PrepareLensedCLSpectra's call AmplitudeError() is commented out (lensing.f90:305-309), and each lensing method returns early when Cphil3(10) > lensing_sanity_check_amplitude (lines 448, 785, 1016, 1194), leaving the zero-initialised Cl_lensed. The high-L template normalisation check (328) is an empty if-block. The rationale 'some theories really destroy the Cls' does not need a silent skip, since the error is already catchable. AmplitudeError is now dead code.
- **Proposed fix (unknown):** Restore call AmplitudeError() in PrepareLensedCLSpectra, delete the early-return blocks (callers already return on global_error_flag), and restore the high-L template check as a GlobalError. If silent skipping is wanted for MCMC, make it opt-in and set a global error message.

### F-135 [S3, unverified] CalcScalarSources transfer-time mismatch: EFTCAMB's hard error only fires when print_fortran_warnings is on, has no message, and also aborts GR runs

- **Location:** `fortran/cmbmain.f90:1189`
- **Track:** t8b-integration; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** Merged condition: if (abs(dtau) > 5e-5 .and. abs(dtau)/tau > 5e-5 .and. print_fortran_warnings) then write WARNING; global_error_flag = 1; return. Code reading; no input that triggers the mismatch was found.
- **Expected:** The error decision independent of a printing flag, with a global_error_message so Python shows the cause (not 'Error code: 1'). GR behaviour as upstream (warning only), unless the stricter check is intended for all models.
- **Root cause:** Upstream v2 added '.and. print_fortran_warnings' to the warning condition. The rebase kept the pre-rebase EFTCAMB error-and-return inside the same if. Setting config.print_fortran_warnings=False therefore suppresses the error and the run continues with mismatched transfer times. Setting the shared global_error_flag from inside the OpenMP k loop has no message.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** if (abs(dt) > 5e-5_dl .and. abs(dt)/tau > 5e-5_dl) then; if (print_fortran_warnings) write(*,*) 'WARNING: mismatch in integrated times (CAMB: CalcScalarSources)'; if (CP%EFTCAMB%EFTflag /= 0) then; call GlobalError('EFTCAMB: mismatch in integrated times (CalcScalarSources)', error_evolution); return; end if; end if

### F-136 [S3, unverified] ThermoData_Init doubles the background sampling only for non-LCDM CP%DarkEnergy; EFT backgrounds (placeholder w=-1) never get it

- **Location:** `fortran/results.f90:2208`
- **Track:** t8b-integration; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/checks/t8b-integration/ninv_reference.py`
- **Observed:** Doubling ninverse also for EFTflag/=0 (73-model batch vs A0): Kmimic_2 P(k) 3.7e-2, Kmimic_1 4.5e-3, hdsk_TG_2 7.2e-4, Kmimic_3 2.5e-4, ADE_1 2.8e-5, all others <= 9e-6. Against a BackgroundTimeStepBoost=4 reference neither version is better (Kmimic_1 worst_cl 5.79e-2 vs 5.80e-2): these models' error is dominated by other unconverged parts (Kmimic_2 is known unconverged).
- **Expected:** EFT models with dynamical DE should get the same background-sampling treatment CAMB gives non-LCDM dark energy.
- **Root cause:** 'if (.not. CP%DarkEnergy%is_cosmological_constant) ninverse = ninverse*2' reads the never-initialised DarkEnergy placeholder (Init is skipped for EFT, so is_cosmological_constant stays .true.).
- **Proposed fix (physics-changing):** if (.not. CP%DarkEnergy%is_cosmological_constant .or. CP%EFTCAMB%EFTflag /= 0) ninverse = ninverse*2 (guard allocated(CP%EFTCAMB)). Tested in a copy of A0 (diff kept in work/t8b-integration/out/experiment_ninverse.diff).

### F-137 [S3, unverified] EFTCAMB Makefile changes: unconditional -fallow-argument-mismatch (breaks gfortran 6-9 that setup.py accepts), -w silences all warnings, -DDEBUG in Debug builds, link libs in compile flags, ineffective dependency rules

- **Location:** `fortran/Makefile:117` (also: fortran/Makefile:55-61, fortran/Makefile:69, fortran/Makefile:123-124, fortran/Makefile_main:49-74)
- **Track:** t8b-integration; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** 1) COMMON_FFLAGS adds -fallow-argument-mismatch (gfortran >= 10 only) for every source, while camb/_compilers.py gfortran_min = '6': gfortran 6-9 fail with an unknown-option error, and real argument mismatches in CAMB code are hidden. 2) -lblas -llapack inside COMMON_FFLAGS (compile flags). 3) FFLAGS = -O3 -w silences all compiler warnings in release builds. 4) DEBUGFLAGS (gfortran and ifort) add -DDEBUG, so 'make Debug' switches on DebugEFTCAMB (cache-file IO, ThreadNum=1, extra DEBUG time steps in results.f90:2844, STOPs), and Debug and Release no longer compute the same thing. 5) ifort DEBUGFLAGS use -fpe2 and -mkl alongside -qmkl. 6) Makefile_main:49-74 dependency rules name unprefixed objects (real targets are Release*/x.o), and $(RECOMBINATION_FILES).o etc. only suffix the last word of a list.
- **Expected:** Flags safe for every supported compiler; warnings visible; Debug builds equal to Release apart from checks; dependency rules that match the real targets.
- **Root cause:** Build tweaks carried over from the pre-rebase EFTCAMB, applied globally instead of to the bundled odepack sources or an EFT-specific debug target.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Add -fallow-argument-mismatch only for gfortran major >= 10 (compiler_ver test) and preferably only for the EFTCAMB odepack .f objects. Keep BLAS/LAPACK in LIBLINK only. Drop -w (or limit it to eftcamb sources). Use a separate EFTDEBUG target/variable for -DDEBUG. Restore -fpe0 and -qmkl for ifort. Delete the hand-written dependency chain (the -MMD .d files already give the order) or prefix it with $(OUTPUT_DIR)/ and $(DLL_DIR)/.

### F-138 [S3, unverified] Debug/dead code in the CAMB integration: commented-out fixq_array loop, STOP in library code, 'denser' grid that is coarser, cumulative dtaurec division, no-op atol line, stale comments, 21cm error stop

- **Location:** `fortran/cmbmain.f90:198` (also: fortran/cmbmain.f90:265, fortran/cmbmain.f90:1131, fortran/cmbmain.f90:1306, fortran/results.f90:2848, fortran/results.f90:2941, fortran/camb.f90:132, fortran/camb.f90:918, fortran/lensing.f90:205)
- **Track:** t8b-integration; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** cmbmain.f90:198-217: in DebugEFTCAMB with fixq_array set, Evolve_q is re-initialised and the loop that should add the k ranges is fully commented out (empty k list). cmbmain.f90:265 and 1131: 'stop' in library code (kills Python in -Dfixq builds). results.f90:2941/2944: nri0 = nri0/10._dl under 'denser grid for fixq' makes the reionization grid 10x coarser. results.f90:2848/2852: State%dtaurec divided by 10 each time the time steps are set (cumulative on repeated calls); the '#if defined(fixq) || !defined(fixq_array)' block adds extra time steps to every DEBUG build. cmbmain.f90:1306: 'if (high_precision) atol = atol' is a no-op (measured effect of dropping atol/100 for EFT: <= 1.44e-5 in P(k), transfer-only k). camb.f90:132-136: 'Original code' comment is wrong, since upstream v2 already calls SetParams(P) there. lensing.f90:205: AmplitudeError unused. camb.f90:918: 21cm + EFT is 'error stop', which kills the interpreter when reached via camb.run_ini.
- **Expected:** Working or removed debug code; errors through GlobalError; comments matching the code.
- **Root cause:** Pre-rebase debug scaffolding and merge leftovers.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Remove or repair the fixq_array block. Replace stop/error stop with GlobalError + return. Use nri0 = nri0*10 if a denser grid is intended. Use a local copy instead of mutating State%dtaurec. Replace the no-op with 'if (CP%Transfer%high_precision .and. CP%EFTCAMB%EFTflag == 0) atol = atol/100' (or restore upstream, effect <= 1.4e-5). Fix the stale comment. Delete AmplitudeError or reinstate it (see lensing finding). Turn the 21cm check into ErrMsg + return.

### F-139 [S3, unverified] H0 search range for theta: the EFTCAMB MOD lower bound also applies to GR, and the public set_H0_for_theta default range is unusable with EFT models

- **Location:** `camb/model.py:785` (also: fortran/camb.f90:315)
- **Track:** t8c-features; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Reproduce:** `/Users/marco/Software/miniforge3/bin/python /Users/marco/IW/eftcamb_update/EFTCAMB/fortran/eftcamb_test/audit/repro/theta-h0-range-eft.py`
- **Observed:** (1) Designer f(R) LCDM: cp.set_H0_for_theta(0.010411) with the default (10,100) range aborts with CAMBError 'EFTCAMB: theory unstable', evaluated at H0=10. With range (55,100) it finds H0=63.8294. c7: 6 of 7 EFT models abort this way.
(2) GR, EFTflag=0, Omega_m(H0=58)=0.96: set_cosmology(thetastar=...) gives 'No solution for H0 inside of theta_H0_range' on A0. Plain CAMB v2 (B0) recovers H0=58.0004.
- **Expected:** GR keeps upstream behaviour, and set_H0_for_theta either works with EFT models or reports out-of-range instead of aborting.
- **Root cause:** The EFTCAMB MOD in set_cosmology (model.py:785-794) raises the lower bracket to 1.1*100*sqrt(omega_m) for every model, including EFTflag=0 and omk/=0. set_H0_for_theta itself, and Fortran CAMB_SetH0ForTheta (camb.f90:315), keep (10,100), where EFT backgrounds with Omega_DE<0 raise CAMBError, which propagates out of brentq. This is the same mechanism as the t8b finding theta-solve-aborts-unstable-bracket-end.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Apply the min_H0 bracket only when self.EFTCAMB.EFTflag != 0. Move it into set_H0_for_theta (and CAMB_SetH0ForTheta) so the public method and the ini path get it too. Treat a CAMBError at a trial H0 as 'outside the valid range', e.g. return +-inf to brentq or shrink the bracket.

### F-140 [S3, unverified] F-001 follow-up: getters write into immutable bytes objects, multibyte model names break model_name(), -fcheck aborts depend on chance

- **Location:** `camb/eftcamb.py:267` (also: camb/eftcamb.py:165, camb/eftcamb.py:228, camb/eftcamb.py:243, camb/eftcamb.py:277, fortran/eftcamb/11_EFTCAMB_python.f90:157,212,226,253,270)
- **Track:** t9-wrapper; reported severity S3, confidence high
- **Observed:** The affected interfaces are character(len=200) dummies called with no hidden length: get_read_parameters (key, value; l.157), EFTCAMB_get_param_names (l.212), EFTCAMB_get_param_labels (l.226), EFTCAMB_get_model_name (l.253) and EFTCAMB_set_model_name (l.270). initialize_EFTCAMB_from_py_read_params is bind(c) with explicit lengths and is fine. On A0 (release) no overflow is possible: every Python buffer is exactly 200 bytes. However, the Python bytes object passed to get_model_name is modified in place (bytes_object_mutated=True). set_model_name('Omega'+'Ω'*150) is cut at 200 bytes mid-character, and model_name() then raises UnicodeDecodeError. On AD, model_name and set_model_name abort (127/200) while param_names, param_labels and read_parameters pass only because the register holding the missing length happens to be large. Passing c_size_t(200) hidden lengths makes all 5 correct on AD.
- **Expected:** Mutable buffers (create_string_buffer), explicit string lengths, and byte-safe truncation.
- **Root cause:** camb/eftcamb.py builds immutable bytes ('{:<200}'.format(..).encode()) and omits the hidden length that gfortran and ifort append for character dummies. Names are padded by characters, not bytes.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Best: give these 5 routines bind(c) interfaces with explicit lengths (as initialize_EFTCAMB_from_py_read_params already has). Minimal: use create_string_buffer(200), append c_size_t(200) per string argument (needed for ifort too), and truncate set_model_name input to 200 bytes on a character boundary (decode with errors='replace'). See fix_python.diff (_hidden helper).

### F-141 [S3, unverified] Python wrapper input hygiene: spaces stripped inside values, 200-char value truncation, silent zero defaults, uninformative error, shared 'cs2' key

- **Location:** `fortran/eftcamb/11_EFTCAMB_python.f90:95` (also: fortran/eftcamb/09_EFTCAMB_main.f90:149-150, camb/eftcamb.py:146, fortran/eftcamb/08f_full_models/008p2_ADE.f90:181)
- **Track:** t9-wrapper; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** (a) helper_stripspaces removes all internal spaces of keys and values, so '1 2' becomes '12' and an output_root with spaces is mangled. No current EFT key takes a list. (b) read_param_values is character(len=200): a 239-char value comes back with 200 chars. (c) A missing model parameter (EFTOmega0 omitted) gives no warning, EFTOmega0 silently 0, and the model runs. (d) Every model-selection error raises ValueError('EFTCAMB error during initialization') with the reason printed only to stdout. (e) 'cs2' is both a CAMB set_params keyword (DarkEnergy) and the ADE sound speed key. With EFT on, the results are identical to the ini path (TT maxrel 0.0, DarkEnergy.cs2 also set to 0.5), so this is benign today.
- **Expected:** Values passed through verbatim (trim only), no silent truncation, a warning for unread or defaulted model parameters, and the error reason in the exception message.
- **Root cause:** helper_stripspaces (l.44-62) is applied to values; 09_EFTCAMB_main.f90:149-150 uses fixed-length storage; Ini%Read_Double(key, 0) defaults are used for model parameters; the error message is generic.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** Use trim(adjustl()) instead of helper_stripspaces for values. Store read values as allocatable deferred-length strings (or warn on truncation). Have EFTCAMB_init_model_from_file report the failing key in a global_error_message that the Python side includes in the ValueError. Document the 'cs2' sharing.

### F-142 [S3, unverified] With EFTflag=0, read_parameters() returns only EFTflag, so set_params(EFTflag=0, <other EFT key>) raises CAMBUnknownArgumentError

- **Location:** `camb/camb.py:224`
- **Track:** user-report; reported severity S3, confidence medium
- **Observed:** set_params(EFTflag=0, EFT_additional_priors=...) raises CAMBUnknownArgumentError (reported by the user, 2026-10-19); the pre-rebase code hid it through a stale global parameter cache.
- **Expected:** EFT keys accepted (and ignored) when EFTflag=0, as they are by the ini path.
- **Root cause:** set_params marks as used only the keys returned by read_parameters, which lists only EFTflag when no model is allocated.
- **Proposed fix (safe-local):** In EFT_do_set, also mark as used every key the EFTCAMB ini reader recognises (or every key starting with EFT/known model prefixes) when EFTflag=0.

## Checks per track

### t0-layout: FINDINGS

The ctypes layouts match the Fortran types exactly. I generated a Fortran program from the Python _fields_, compiled it against the A0 modules and linked it to camblib.so. It checked 516 mapped components: offset, size and Fortran type/kind (via select type), plus allocatable descriptors found by allocating them and scanning the parent's memory. There were 0 mismatches on A0, and the same on AD. Types checked: TEFTCAMB_timestep_cache (1272 B = ctypes; array stride 1272), TEFTCAMB_parameter_cache (272 = 272), TEFTCAMB (python maps the first 168 B of 328; unmapped tail is outroot, model, read_param_keys/values), CAMBparams (3032 = 3032; EFTCAMB at 3000 and eft_par_cache at 3016 match the python tail), TransferParams, AccuracyParams, SourceTermParams, CustomSources, and the python-mapped CAMBdata prefix. I also parsed the component lists from the Fortran source: python maps exactly the Fortran components, in order, so no component hides in alignment padding. Logical fields go through BoolField as c_int (4 B), which matches logical(4). Allocatable sizes 16/64/64 match. Value round trips for designer f(R), hdsk_TG_1 and K-mouflage_1 all pass: parameter-cache fields equal the CAMBdata ctypes fields and the analytic densities to <=4.6e-16; get_eft_functions echoes a and tau exactly; adotoa equals a H(z)/c to 5.5e-16; species densities follow the a-scalings to 2.3e-16; finite differences of 26 derivative-field pairs confirm the field order (worst 1.75e-2, K-mouflage interpolation); f(R) B0 rebuilt from EFTOmegaV/P, adotoa and Hdot equals EFTB0 to 1.9e-12. The get_evolution output is [k][time], matching the Fortran fill order (k echo exact, adotoa equals get_eft_functions to 3e-8). The TEFTCAMB echo passes in both directions (ini to python, 3 models; python to Fortran feedback, 18 fields).  Defects found: (S1) reusing results.Params, or calling results.get_BAO(z), which CAMB v2 now defaults to self.Params.copy(), aborts the process at results.f90:580, an allocate of an already allocated array. (S1) pickle, deepcopy and repr/eval of CAMBparams drop the unmapped TEFTCAMB%model; get_background on the restored parameters then dies with SIGSEGV. Same in B1. (S2) get_evolution sorts eta silently, so decreasing a or unsorted eta gives misaligned rows. (S2) the full-mapping Hdotdotdot formula is wrong: 223x off for K-mouflage_1, 822x for K-mouflage_2; designer f(R) leaves it at 0. It feeds the positivity bounds and the python output, not the Cls. Plus S3 hygiene items. The three safe-local fixes were tested in a rebuilt tree copy: the crashes are gone, the evolution order is restored, and Cls/P(k)/EFT outputs are bit-identical to A0 for 3 models. The corrected Hdotdotdot derivation was checked in python: it matches the numerical derivative to <=1.9e-3.

### t1-crash: FINDINGS

I built runtime-checked copies of tree AD (gfortran -O0 -fcheck=all, snan init, F-002 patched) and ran all 85 inis through Python with FP traps enabled via _gfortran_set_fpe(13): the 73 test models plus the 12 audit inis covering the 6 models that have no test ini. I also ran the checked command-line executable on 17 models, ran the DebugEFTCAMB (ADD) path, and compiled with -Wall -Wextra -Wconversion and related flags. Apart from F-002, no trip was upstream-only. Every trap site was traced to a file:line, either with lldb or with dSYM symbolisation.  With a set of safe-local patches applied, 75 of 85 inis complete under -fcheck=all plus FP traps. The other 10 stop for reasons that are by design or are symptoms of defects other tracks have filed: - 4 RPH models: division by zero at 007p2_RPH.f90:1655 inside compute_additional_derivs (t2d additional-derivs-wrong-cache). - 2 inis for 3+1 wDE (007p8): the stability check refuses the model by design. - 2 Beyond Horndeski inis: 0/0 at 008p5:1017 because adotoa = 0 when called from dtauda (another track's beyond-horndeski-hubble). - 2 Extended Galileon inis: overflow in the RK45 combination step, because derivatives reach -1.55e307 right after EFT turn-on at a = 0.0100001. In the release build this model fails with RK45 error -3 (Extended Galileon background findings by other tracks).  Every output from the runs that completed is finite: 77 npz files and all CLI .dat files. The command-line and Python entry points give the same outcomes once the CLI SetParams defect is patched.  New defects from this track: - S2: get_eft_functions crashes Python with SIGSEGV when no EFTCAMB cache was initialised (EFTflag=0, or get_results with nothing to compute). - S2: out-of-bounds read in the equispaced interpolation at x_final, reachable with a shipped ini. - S2: the command-line exe segfaults in SetParams for every model when built at -O0, because a non-short-circuit .and. tests the old parameter set (results.f90:365). - S3: the EV flags are garbage in the OpenMP PRIVATE copies; this caused 21 of the checked-build traps. - S3: uninitialised locals in derivs and derivst (w_dark_energy_t trapped 50 models; grhog_t/grhor_t trap every tensor run). - S3: FP exceptions inside dead or discarded code (RSA z/dz for hdsk with evolve_delta_phi, quintessence y(2), Beyond Horndeski hpprime). - S3: hygiene items flagged by compiler warnings. - S3: defects in the debug-only code path (fort.6666, output from different threads interleaved, the broken fixq_array branch, stop statements).  All safe-local fixes applied together, in release tree relS, leave all 73 models at the A0 thread-count noise floor: worst Cl of 1.9e-14, the same value an A0 rerun gives, and zero change in P(k), sigma8 and tensor BB.

### t10a-regression: FINDINGS

I compared the code under audit (A0) with pre-rebase EFTCAMB (B1) and legacy outputs (B2) for all 73 test models, and found no regression specific to EFT. Method: for each spectrum, the double ratio (A0/B1)_model / (A0/B1)_GR - 1, using the best-matched EFTflag=0 reference (DR below). For EFTflag=0 references with tabulated w: quintessence, mc5e designer, ADE, wCDM and CPL. 37 models have DR < 1e-4 in TT/EE/TE/BB/PP/P(k). Quintessence and mc5e drop to 1e-6 once the background matches; with an LCDM reference they were up to 5e-2. The other 36 models fall into four classes, each traced to an inherited CAMB v2 change: (A) default-accuracy differences; (L) interpolation between sampled multipoles; (N) massive-neutrino changes; plus the Fortran lensing method, which accounts for low-ell lensed BB. Class A covers PEFT Omega/gamma3/gamma4_const/gamma5, RPH, f(R), Horava_eta, K-mimic. With both trees at accuracy_boost=2, l_accuracy_boost=2, IntTolBoost=2 and massless neutrinos, unlensed DR is <=2e-4 at ell>30 (Horava_eta_4 2e-3, not converged at default); residual is <=4e-3, only at ell<30. B1 at l_accuracy_boost=1 is under-resolved: lAccuracyBoost=2 alone cuts the 2_PEFT_Omega_const_1 EE DR from 2.3e-2 to 4.9e-3. Low-ell lensed-BB DR (1.4e-2) is the Fortran lensing method: a common Python lensing code gives 2.8e-6. Class L covers Horndeski TG and K-mouflage. Their deviations do not shrink with accuracy, but with l_sample_boost=50 in both trees TG_1 tensor BB drops from 1.3e-2 to 1.8e-4 and K-mouflage_2 EE from 2e-2 to 2e-3. Class N is 5_scg: tensor/TT DR vanishes without massive neutrinos. Localisation: the EFT module code is unchanged at statement level (renames, default initialisers, string splits). The EFTCAMB hook blocks in derivs, derivst, outputt, initialt, tight coupling and the ICs are equivalent. With massless neutrinos, solver-level EFT functions agree to 1e-16. The eft_* output differences (Hdotdot 6-14%, EFTLambdadot, Horava Gamma1P) come from a bug in B1's get_eft_functions that A0 fixes; A0's Hdotdot matches the numerical derivative to 2e-6. B1's massive-neutrino pressure derivatives were also wrong (dP3 off by 200%), but a B1 build with A0's derivatives gives the same spectra. Legacy B2 is effectively B1's output: raw <=4e-4 except the K-mimic P(k) jump at k~0.21/Mpc. So A0 vs B2 equals A0 vs B1. Findings: (1) S2, not a regression (same code in B1): the full-mapping third Hubble derivative formula is wrong (off by up to 300x; a corrected formula, checked numerically, is proposed). (2) S3: an EFTCAMB hook silently forces IntTolBoost>=2 for EFTflag!=0, so the exact-GR EFT run no longer reproduces EFTflag=0. (3) S3: uninitialised locals in derivst. (4) S3 upstream: get_background segfaults with source windows and WantCls=F. Not resolved: the 1e-4 criterion at ell<30, and models without test inis.

### t10b-convergence: FINDINGS

I ran the test models on A0 at default accuracy and at accuracy_boost 2 and 3 (l_accuracy_boost and l_sample_boost set to the same value, lens_potential_accuracy left at its default). To keep boost-3 runs affordable I switched off the redshift windows (num_redshiftwindows=0); that choice turned out to matter, see below. Coverage: 70 models default vs boost3, 69 boost2 vs boost3, and Kmouflage_1/2 default vs boost2 only. 5_ADE_1 cannot run without windows (the tauminn error already filed by t3), so I compared it with windows at default vs boost2: it is converged (8.4e-4).  GR noise baseline (1_EFT_GR, default vs boost3): unlensed TT 9.6e-4, EE 1.3e-3, TE 9.3e-4, lensing potential 1.3e-3, P(k) 6.0e-4, sigma8 7.9e-5. These numbers are identical in plain upstream CAMB (B0). The total BB/EE differences (6.7e-2 / 1.1e-2) come from tensor Cls not being converged in upstream CAMB v2 itself; that is not EFT.  16 models exceed max(3x GR, 1e-3) at default vs boost3, plus Kmouflage_1/2 at default vs boost2. They fall into four root causes:  (1) The RK45 integrator takes steps longer than the pi oscillation allows, and its error norm does not see pi. Affects Horava_SolSyst_2 (12% at ell 2454), RPH_MB_OmegaDE_1 (7.3e-3), Horava_SolSyst_3 (91%) and Horava_eta_4 (3%). A step limit of 1.5/omega_pi brings SolSyst_2 and RPH_MB_OmegaDE_1 to the GR level. eta_4 converges once the late-time CMB source step is also made finer. SolSyst_3 improves (91% to 11%) but does not converge.  (2) The abrupt EFT turn-on at a=0.01 makes Psi jump by 25-33%. The CMB time quadrature integrates that jump with a first-order error in the source time step. Affects the PEFT Omega_const, gamma3 and gamma5 models, RPH_MK_const_1, Kmouflage_1/2/3 and eta_4: low-ell TT is off by 0.6-7%, and adding the unrelated redshift windows changes it by up to 4.6% (GR: 5e-4). Adding source time steps tightly around the turn-on removes the windows dependence (1.5e-4) and brings default close to the fixed boost3 (Omega_const_1: 1.8e-2 to 3.0e-3).  (3) K-mimic models 1/2/3: one P(k) point near k = 0.001/tau(turn-on), about 0.22/Mpc, is off by a factor of about 3. This happens at every accuracy and also in B1. Cls differ by 30-84% between default and boost3. Root cause is the ill-conditioned type-1 pi' initial condition already found by t5; t5's IC tree removes the glitch and converges these models to the GR level.  (4) New: the type-1 initial pi = -E/(C + k^2 D1 + k^4 D2) has a pole where the mass-plus-gradient coefficient crosses zero. For 3_RPH_MB_const_1 this is at k_c = 1.62e-3/Mpc, where P(k)/P_GR swings between about 15 and 0.004. Regularising the IC removes the pole. About 7% non-convergence remains, from the model's tachyonic band.  Proposed fixes 1 and 2 together change converged models by no more than 3e-5 in most cases (at most 1.4e-3). Of the previously known non-converged models, Horava_SolSyst_1 is in fact converged; SolSyst_2 and SolSyst_3 are not. Four repro scripts were written and run.
