### t2-state: FINDINGS

Track 2 a-c (determinism, state, memory) on A0, resumed from attempt 3; earlier results were confirmed by cheap re-runs.  (a) Threads. 41 models covering every family with a working ini were run on A0 with OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 and =8. - Baseline: 1_EFT_GR gives Cl 5.3e-15 on A0 and 5.0e-15 on B0. - Every model is at that baseline for Cl/P(k)/sigma8/H (worst Cl 8.4e-15, P(k), sigma8 and H bit-identical). No data race. - Same-thread repeats (t1/t1b, t8/t8b/t8c) are bit-identical except fields that are never written. - Three thread-dependent outputs, all localised:   1. EFTcdotdot/EFTGamma1PP in get_scale_evolution are uninitialised memory and change from run to run.   2. V_scf of Horndeski models with designer_V=F is uninitialised heap memory (relative diff ~1 between runs).   3. 008p5 Beyond Horndeski get_scale_evolution depends on the per-thread k order (up to 3.3e-9 relative). The OpenMP-private EV%eft_cache is never reset per k, and BH reads adotoa/Hdot from the cache before they are computed. sNaN poisoning of the private cache turns the whole k=1e-3 evolution into NaN; zero-initialising it per k makes t1/t2/t8 bit-identical.  (b) State. 23 one-process sequences cover: - GR -> EFT -> GR; designer -> full; full -> full; parametrisation changes; nu / no-nu; failing (unstable, bad flag) between good runs; - set_params; reinit of one CAMBparams, including a changed EFT parameter; the same CAMBparams or the same CAMBdata reused; - cp.copy(); getters called repeatedly. Every repeated step is bit-identical to its first occurrence and to a fresh process, except the V_scf garbage. Defects found: - copy.deepcopy and pickle segfault (S1). - Reusing results.Params, e.g. results.get_BAO, aborts Python at results.f90:580 (S1). - Horndeski num_eq is an implicit SAVE (state leaks, outputs unaffected; S3). - Uninitialised cache fields make the stability verdict depend on garbage (S2): a NaN-initialised build falsely rejects 7/10 models; with the fix all pass.  (c) Memory. 300 get_results/get_background iterations alternating 3 EFT models, via read_ini, set_params and in-place reinit, against GR on A0 and B0. - malloc bytes in use stay flat for EFT: -4 to 58 KiB/iter, against 12-87 (A0 GR) and 43-176 (B0 GR). - RSS growth saturates and is the same as in GR and B0. - macOS `leaks` reports 0 leaked blocks in every mode. - initialize_parameters deallocates the model before reallocating it. No EFT leak.  All fixes were tested together in tree work/t2-state/fix. Spectra match the A0 reference to <=5.6e-15 and EFT functions are identical.

### t2d-static: FINDINGS

Track 2d (static sweep: thread safety and numeric hygiene), attempt 4. Attempt 3 had already finished. This attempt reused its results and reran all 9 earlier repros under harness slots with 1 thread; every number came out identical. It then closed the main gap attempt 3 left: a read-before-write scan of the timestep cache over all 43 background routines.  Thread safety: no data race found. Per-k EFT code writes only EV-private caches. The one exception is a lazy allocation of the spline5 yint array, which is latent because it runs serially in all shipped flows. 16 models run with 1 or 2 threads vs 6 threads gave bit-identical unlensed/tensor Cls, P(k), sigma8 and EFT functions; lensed Cls differ by at most 6e-15.  Findings: - S1: Extended Galileon adotoa reads eft_cache%grhonu_tot before any caller fills it. H(a=1)/H0-1 = -6.0e-3 and H jumps by 3.6% at a=0.2; the candidate fix FIX3 brings the error to 1e-14. The bisection tolerance is the integer 10**(-1) = 0. - New S2: for full-mapping models, derivs evaluates the background EFT functions before compute_adotoa/compute_H_derivs and never again. Beyond Horndeski and Extended Galileon read adotoa/Hdot/Hdotdot from the cache, so they use the background of the previous derivs call. In an instrumented BH run EFTc lags by 3.8e-4 and EFTLambdadot by 1.3e-2. The candidate fix changes TT by 2.2e-5 and P(k) by 2.5e-3, against a control of 2e-13 and 3e-5. - Other S2s:   - compute_additional_derivs helpers use the host a/cache. With correct derivatives, 8 of the 14 shipped mass-stability inis flip from stable to unstable.   - QS_sigma is a copy of QS_mu.   - QS_mu/QS_sigma are 0/0 NaN for 7 shipped models.   - The third stability sampling pass re-tests a=astart 1000 times.   - MpiStop/error stop kill the Python interpreter (131 sites, 18 reachable per k).   - The RPH getters use the Tensor_ODE count for the PlanckMass_ODE block. - S3 groups:   - Horndeski implicit-SAVE num_eq.   - Kind-less literals; only SCG's 4.74*1.d-9 changes results above 1e-8 (Cl 1.5e-7).   - Real ==/= comparisons choosing physics branches.   - Undefined results, uninitialised locals and writes to absent optionals.   - Latent thread-safety hygiene.  Harness note: build_tree with fflags=None (MAKEFLAGS=-j4) can break the forutils build and still return ok=True. Use make_args=['-j1','CLUSTER_SAFE=0'] and check the .so timestamp.  Bulky outputs were deleted. The FIX1, FIX2, FIX3 and LAG trees are kept because findings cite them.

### t3-gr: FINDINGS

Part (a), EFTflag=0 on A0 vs plain CAMB v2 (B0): every cause of the difference is found and proven. (1) The reionization max_redshift change (reionization.f90:48, 50 -> 1000) accounts for the whole flat-space difference: 2.37e-5 in EE, 1.6e-5 in BB and 1.1e-6 in TT. Setting max_redshift=50 on A0 leaves 2e-13 (A0 vs B0, and A0 vs B0 with max_redshift=1000, likewise). The change is commented in the source but not documented for users (S3). (2) The rewrite of polterddot (equations.f90:3858) leaves out the curvature factor Kf(2), so low-ell TT is wrong in non-flat GR runs: 4.6e-3 at omk=-0.05, 1.6e-4 at +0.05 (S2). Restoring the Kf(2) factors (tree R3) brings it back to 1e-13. (3) The rewritten Weyl transfer formula is the same algebra and only changes rounding. (4) FMA contraction adds about 1e-13. A0 with max_redshift=50 plus the original polterddot and Weyl formulas, built with -ffp-contract=off (R1nofma), is bit-identical to B0 built the same way (B0nofma) in all 18 variants: nu, nonu, nu3, omk +-0.05, omk -0.01, tensors, lensing on/off, non-linear lensing and P(k), multi-z transfer, small kmax, w=-0.9/-1.1 fluid, w0wa PPF, zre input, theta input, accuracy_boost=2. Other EFTCAMB MOD blocks active at EFTflag=0: the cmbmain 'small fix' (fixes an upstream ERROR STOP for transfer-only runs with kmax<=0.05); the lensing amplitude check silently skips lensing instead of raising an error (S3; A0=B0 for As up to 1.9e-8); and two Python-layer changes. Those two are the theta->H0 bracket (A0 cannot solve omk=-0.2, B0 gives H0=36.3) and P(k) extrapolation (8.7% at kh=100); tracks t8c and t8b already filed them. Part (b), GR points of each model family: Pure EFT, wDE-LCDM, Horava, ShiftSym, the mc quintessence designer and HMcode reproduce EFTflag=0 to 5e-13 (GR path) and 1.2e-8 (EFT path forced on with all functions zero). The scans go smoothly to GR, and switching between the GR and EFT code paths in equations.f90 causes no jump. Horndeski with shooting agrees to 2.9e-6. Defects found: curvature is ignored with EFTflag!=0 (TT off by a factor of ~240), the 3+1 designer can never run, get_scale_evolution segfaults after a P(k)-only run, Extended Galileon has a wrong background, coarse background tables leave a 1e-4 floor (K-mouflage, ADE, quintessence), the RGR switch makes P(k) collapse discontinuously for f(R), RPH has single-k P(k) spikes from a singular pi initial condition, constant Gamma1 is rejected as mass-unstable near GR, the number-counts RSD source leaves out dark energy, the stability check evaluates dtauda without matter (grhoa2 never set), ADE is refused at l_max=2000, set_params(cp=) drops the EFT model, and several S3 hygiene items. Every S1/S2 repro was run in this session. Candidate fixes were tested in trees R3, R5, R6, R7 and R8.

### t4a-param: FINDINGS

I tested all 22 concrete 1D parametrization types (60 parameter sets) and the 3 2D types against their abstract interfaces. A Fortran driver linked against the A0 objects evaluated value, d1-d4 and integral at points from a=1e-8 to a=1, around nodes and steps, and at a>1. Derivatives were checked against 40-digit sympy/mpmath values. The acceptance metric weights the error by a^k, because the EFT functions enter the equations as a^k d^k f/da^k. Finite differences and node continuity were checked as well. Integrals were checked against scipy quad under the EFTCAMB convention I(a)=a^2 exp(-3 int_1^a (1+w)/a' da'), with I(1)=1. I also checked parameter set/get round trips, name counts, feedback, ini keys, the allocator map, the 2D types, AD (-fcheck/FPE-trap) runs and model-level consequences. To get before/after numbers I rebuilt a tree copy with candidate fixes (work/t4a-param/FIX.diff, 7 files).  Analytic types are exact to <=3e-15 (a^k-weighted) except where listed. Defects found, all inherited unchanged from the pre-rebase code (B1): (1) Cubic spline (flag 9): returns null_value at its end nodes, its derivatives disagree with its value, and its integral is broken. Pure-EFT Omega=a written as a spline differs from the analytic Omega=a by 14.6% in Cl (8e-13 after the fix). (2) Steplog (flag 8): the fourth derivative is wrong everywhere and NaN for a>aT exp(delta^2), so a valid Pure-EFT Omega steplog is rejected. integral(1)=1.396 instead of 1, which moves designer f(R) Cl by 3.4e-4. (3) Integrals of both spline types follow the wrong convention. Pade, Fourier and exp2 have no integral at all and call MpiStop (ERROR STOP kills Python). So designer f(R)/ShiftSym with the documented EFTwDE=6,7,9,10,11 options cannot run. (4) Both spline types return null_value instead of 0 for every derivative outside the node range: 1e-4 in Cl, 2e-3 in P(k). (5) The quintic spline evaluates c6*dx^6 instead of c6*dx^5, so the function jumps at nodes (0.93 at a node with Logx). (6) Spline_Order=3, which init accepts, hits ERROR STOP in the 3rd/4th derivative. (7) Ini keys are truncated to 20 characters, so long documented parameter names are silently read as 0 (3+1 designer, RPH *_ODE steplog delta). (8) The Pade fourth derivative is missing a factor. Plus S3 items: Fourier min_a0 with order 0 returns -1e16; spline5 Logx/Logy reports log-transformed parameters; the 2D abstract feedback() segfaults when its optional argument is absent; latent wrong higher derivatives (axion d4=0, step d4 off by 1e9 in dead code, exponential d3/d4 cancellation at a<1e-6).  Spline cases could not be run with -fcheck=bounds: gfortran 16.1 miscompiles them, shown by a minimal valid reproducer.

| Check | Value | Threshold | Pass |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1D derivatives: zero / wDE_LCDM / constant / linear | worst a^k-weighted error <= 1.8e-16 for value and d1-d4; integral vs quad <= 1.8e-14 | value 1e-12, d1 1e-6, d2-d4 1e-4 (a^k-weighted vs 40-digit exact); integral 1e-6 | PASS |
| 1D derivatives: power_law (5 sets incl. negative exponent) | value 1.8e-16, d1 2.9e-16, d2 4.3e-16, d3 5.2e-16, d4 9.1e-16 (pointwise rel <= 3.1e-15); integral 4.3e-14 | as above | PASS |
| 1D derivatives: exponential (flag 4) | weighted: value 1.2e-15, d1 1.8e-16, d2 1.6e-16, d3 5.5e-16, d4 3.0e-15. Pointwise rel fails at a~1e-8 only: d3 1.0, d4 9.1e11 (code 0 vs exact 3.0 for exponent 2), due to cancellation in the factored formula | as above | PASS |
| 1D derivatives: taylorseries (flag 5) | <= 6.9e-16 all methods (pointwise rel <= 5.7e-15) | as above | PASS |

### t4b-derivs: FINDINGS

I checked every time-derivative relation in the EFTCAMB timestep cache numerically on A0. The method: get_eft_functions on 9-point stencils in ln a, three step sizes, 8th-order central differences, 240 centres in a = 1e-7..0.92. This covers 72 of the 73 test models; 1_EFT_GR can't be queried because get_eft_functions segfaults for EFTflag=0, which another track already filed. On top of that I ran the generic background identities, one-sided checks at the perturbation turn-on time a_on (after RGR), offline formula checks and a symbolic sympy check. I also checked consistency between the cache filled inside derivs and the getter.  Conventions used: - adotoa is the conformal Hubble rate; Hdot, Hdotdot and Hdotdotdot are d/dtau; alpha*dot, dphi and sijdot are d/dtau. - The P/PP/PPP suffixes are d/da. - a^2-weighted quantities (EFTc, EFTLambda, gpres*m_t, grhonu/gpinu, grhoq/gpresq) store a^2 d(X/a^2)/dtau = dX/dtau - 2 adotoa X.  These conventions hold across all models. The tests that use the alternative convention fail at O(1).  **What passes:** - Most designer models (pure EFT, RPH, mc5e) pass every first and higher derivative at the 1e-7 level. - All Gamma3/4/5/6 derivatives and all alpha_i derivatives pass (at most 1.5e-7). So do Horndeski's phi derivatives and sij, and gpresdotm_t and gpinudot/dotdot. - Conservation of baryons, CDM, photons and massless neutrinos holds to 3e-13. - adotoa matches CAMB's own H(z) to 1e-15 for every model except SCG. - The cache filled in derivs agrees with get_eft_functions to at most 1e-12.  **Defects found, all present in B1 as well (not regressions):** - **Horava (S1):** Hdot and Hdotdot are inconsistent with its own adotoa, and Hdotdotdot is never set. TT changes by up to 47% at l≤30 when this is fixed. - **Scaling Cubic Galileon (S1):** the background ignores massive neutrinos. Its adotoa is 7% (early times) and 3.5% (late times) off CAMB's H. With a candidate fix, TT changes by about 13% and sigma8 goes from 0.629 to 0.670. - **Scaling Cubic Galileon third derivatives (S2):** wrong hpprime, fpprime, EFTLambdadotdot and EFTGamma2PPP. The fix is verified symbolically and in a patched build; spectra are unchanged. - **Full-mapping models and designer f(R) (S2):** they leave EFTcdotdot, EFTLambdadotdot, EFTGamma1PP and EFTOmegaPPPP at zero, and the generic Hdotdotdot formula is wrong. These feed the positivity/Minkowski priors. This overlaps the t0-layout and t6 repros. - **Two small S3 items:** the upstream CAMB v2 massive-neutrino fits (rho, p and drho) are mutually inconsistent at about 1e-4 of rho_nu; and an approximate massive-neutrino delta p in dgpnu.  **Turn-on:** 53 of 72 models pass every resolved relation at a_on. What enters the initial conditions is the Horava Hdot error (3e-2 for eta_4 and SolSyst_3) and the SCG adotoa error (7e-2). The higher-derivative fields that fail at a_on do not enter the initial conditions.  **Numerical floors (not filed, explained in checks):** - Designer RPH/f(R) tables: Friedmann residual up to 2.7e-6. - mc5e: DE conservation residual up to 1.2e-4, from interpolating the rho_DE table. - Full-mapping tables (quintessence, ADE, K-mouflage): conformal time vs 1/adotoa off by up to 4.7e-5. This converges to 2e-7 with 8x model_background_num_points. - SCG at a≈6e-6: errors around 1e-3, from linear interpolation of its tables. - Horndeski TG Gamma1P: noise around 1e-4 of the size of its two terms. Gamma1 itself is zero analytically, so this comes from cancellation.  Perturbation-level dot fields read from get_scale_evolution can't be verified at 1e-6. The noise floor is 1e-5 to 1e-4, and the large outliers come from issues other tracks already filed (pidotdot read before it is computed; get_evolution noise).

### t5-robust: FINDINGS

Track 5 (numerical robustness) is finished. The noise floor is accuracy_boost 2 vs 1: lensed Cl about 1.1e-2 (low ell), TT at ell>=30 up to 7.5e-4, P(k) up to 2e-3, sigma8 up to 7.6e-5. These values are the same for GR, so the noise comes from CAMB, not from EFT.  Each of the four control parameters was multiplied and divided by 10: - EFTCAMB_stability_time: no effect at all (changes of order 1e-15). - EFTCAMB_back_turn_on: changes appear only in models whose background is modified before a = 1e-7 (Horndeski TG, scg, Kmouflage), and only at k > 20/Mpc. This is physical. - EFTCAMB_turn_on_time: the large changes (54 models at x10, 21 at /10) are all in models whose return-to-GR time equals the turn-on, i.e. models that are not GR-like there, and the code prints a warning for them. This is documented behaviour. The exception is K-mimic, where the dependence comes from a defect in the initial conditions. - EFTCAMB_GR_threshold: the return-to-GR test is badly designed. Raising the threshold to 1e-7 switches the dark-energy perturbations off for minimally coupled DE (sigma8 changes by 0.6-0.7%, low-ell TT by 6-8%). For f(R) the threshold moves P(k) at k >= 1/Mpc by 2-11%.  Initial conditions: - The EFT_IC_type=1 initial pi' has a typo: D1 is used where D2 belongs. This makes K-mimic P(k) wrong by tens of percent to more than 100% at k >= 2/Mpc. - That same pi' also takes finite-difference derivatives of pi coefficients that vary by orders of magnitude between neighbouring a, which puts pi far off its attractor. Effects: K-mimic results depend on the turn-on time and blow up by 1e8 at turn-on 1e-9 with no error, and the Horndeski delta_phi and pi formulations disagree by 2.2e-4 instead of 7e-7. - EFT_IC_type 2 and 3 are unusable except at very early turn-on. They give garbage (up to 1e20), NaN or integrator errors for 13 of 17 models. They also read a stale or uninitialised cache, and IC3 returns NaN for every model with Omega = Gamma3 = 0.  Flag toggles: - EFTCAMB_evolve_metric_h=T starts from the GR h', which violates the Horndeski constraint: P(k) is off by 13% at k = 10/Mpc. A candidate fix reduces this to 4e-4. - EFTCAMB_use_background agrees within the noise floor.  There are two minor findings: misleading return-to-GR feedback, and three Gamma4 test inis that actually run LCDM. Every S1/S2 finding has a repro script that I ran.

### t6-stability: FINDINGS

I audited the stability decision logic on A0 and tested candidate fixes in tree copies. The logic lives in 09_EFTCAMB_stability.f90, the stability and positivity coefficients in 06_abstract_EFTCAMB_model.f90, the model priors and the positivity allowlist in camb/camb.py.  What works: six boundary scans with generic conditions agree with independently computed analytic boundaries to within 1e-4. The scans cover RPH alpha_K, alpha_B ghost, alpha_B gradient, alpha_T and M*, plus Pure EFT gamma1. This agreement needs the analytic side restricted to a >= model_background_a_ini. Every instability raises a clean CAMBError, and the same Python process then reproduces a fresh-process Cl bit for bit. Horava priors behave correctly. Designer f(R) with B0 < 0 is caught when EFT_mass_math_stability is on.  Problems, 7 at S1/S2, each with a repro I ran: - S1: the scalar no-ghost coefficient EFT_kinetic puts the 6 H gamma2 Omega' term inside an h0^2 bracket; it should carry one h0. With a running Planck mass it is off by a factor up to 6.3, and an RPH model that is a ghost at all times is accepted. - S2: the third sampling pass never updates y (duplicate of F-012). My repro shows a narrow ghost window near a = 1 being missed. - S2: the Minkowski tachyonic condition is evaluated with EFTLambdadotdot = 0, because positivity bounds are computed before compute_additional_derivs. The Python guard does not cover EFT_minkowski_limit either. - S2: the EFT_mass_stability verdict cannot be trusted. It rejects canonical quintessence (\|mu\|/H^2 ~ 1e9). The 15 shipped inis that enable it pass only because the derivatives are zeroed (F-009); with correct derivatives 8 of them fail. - S2: catastrophic cancellation in EFT_kinetic at a < 7e-6 makes a healthy RPH model a spurious ghost at the default EFTCAMB_stability_time. - S2: EFTcdotdot and EFTGamma1PP are never initialised but are NaN-checked, so the verdict depends on stack contents. A NaN-initialised build rejects 27 of 73 models. This overlaps another track's uninit-cache-fields-garbage. - S2: the stability cache carries 3x the massive-neutrino pressure, so Hdotdot is off by up to 6.8%.  There are 5 grouped S3 items: allowlist inconsistencies, the unused EFTCAMB_stability_threshold, sampler resolution, the checked interval not being tied to the model's valid or integrated range, and reporting hygiene.

### t8a-equations: FINDINGS

I checked all 65 EFTCAMB MOD blocks in fortran/equations.f90 at HEAD (5bef49a). The rebase from CAMB 1.6.5 to CAMB v2 was done mechanically, and none of the problems found was introduced by it. I replayed the formatted pre-rebase EFT changes onto formatted upstream CAMB v2 with a 3-way merge. That merge differs from HEAD only in renames (EFTFlag->EFTflag, W%->w%, ThermalNuBack->ThermalNuBackground, compute_Einstein_Factors->_factors), added MOD markers and comments, and 10 merge conflicts resolved equivalently (MassiveNuVars dgp argument, redshift-window sources, nu_l_accuracy_boost in initial()). Upstream's own changes to derivs, derivst and output between 1db4141 and camb are cosmetic only. The meaningful upstream changes elsewhere are the dverk->RungeKuttaDP45 switch with first-same-as-last derivative reuse, massive-neutrino accuracy boosts and new isocurvature initial conditions. The variable switches, EFT turn-on included, reset first_derivative_valid. The indices of the EFT variables (EV%w_ix, +1, +2) are used consistently in SetupScalarArrayIndices, CopyScalarVariableArray, the initial conditions and derivs. With EFTflag=0, EFTCAMB moves the dark-energy variables to the front of the y vector; for fluid and PPF dark energy this still matches plain CAMB v2 to 5e-12. Flat GR with EFTflag=0 matches CAMB v2 to 8e-16 once Reion.max_redshift is set to 50, which confirms that the known 2.4e-5 GR difference comes from the reionization setting. All the defects below already exist in the pre-rebase code. (1) S1: in derivs, pi'' is read from ayprime before the pi equation writes it, so EFTeomM and EFTeomNdot use pi''=0 (or a stale value). The ISW source, sigma'', PsiDot and the window ISW terms then miss k(Gamma4+2Gamma5)pi''/(1+Omega). For Horava, TT changes by 4.6e-3 (eta_4), 2.3e-4 (eta_3) and 2.6% at low ell / 1% at ell=1000 (SolSyst_3). A one-place fix reproduces the corrected result to 6e-16 and leaves unaffected models bit-identical. (2) S2: EFTCAMB_evolve_metric_h=T gives -1.1% TT at ell 2-10, -13% P(k) at k=10 and x13 at k=100 compared with the constraint-based and pi-field versions of the same model (those two agree to about 1e-7). The error does not shrink at accuracy_boost=2 and B1 shows the same. (3) S2, also filed by t3: the EFT polterddot replacement drops the curvature factor Kf(2), so EFTflag=0 non-flat runs differ from CAMB by up to 5.4e-3 at ell=2. (4) S3: locals read before they are set (w_dark_energy_t in derivs for EFT models; grhog_t/grhor_t in derivst, also filed by t1). Both trap in an SNaN build; the release-build effect measured as 0. (5) S3: dead or redundant code and a units mismatch in the radiation-streaming (RSA) branch. (6) S3: the massive-neutrino pressure perturbation is approximated as w*delta_rho.

### t8b-integration: FINDINGS

I reviewed all 75 EFTCAMB MOD blocks in my track (cmbmain 13, results 12, camb 10, lensing 6, halofit 3, model 2, massive_neutrinos 2, reionization 1, Makefile 5, Makefile_main 7, plus 9 Python blocks in camb.py, model.py and results.py). Each block was compared with the pre-rebase code (1db4141 to 76b5698) and with upstream's changes (1db4141 to 1d1d3d6).  For EFTflag=0 the integration blocks change nothing except the reionization default. With Reion.max_redshift set back to 50, A0 matches plain CAMB v2 (B0) to 1.4e-13 in the Cls. The EFTCAMB change of max_redshift (50 to 1000) fully explains the known A0-vs-B0 difference of 2.3e-5 in EE: the tau-to-z_re bisection starts from a different bracket, so z_re comes out as 10.958642 instead of 10.958691.  Background routing when EFTflag != 0: - Routed correctly: H(z), distances, conformal-time tables, tau0, age, r_s/r_drag, BAO, the thermal history and recombination (all go through the EFT dtauda, or the EFT tables when EFTCAMB_use_background=T). Comoving distance agrees with a quadrature of c/H to 3e-7 or better. - Bypassed: get_background_densities, get_Omega and get_dark_energy_rho_w use the uninitialised w=-1 DarkEnergy placeholder (H mismatch up to 5.4%). cosmomc_theta with EFTCAMB_use_background=T uses the exact r_s (theta off by 1.0e-3). For Horndeski models the H0 of the EFT background never reaches CP%H0 (sigma8 off by 12%, theta-to-H0 solve broken). The ninverse doubling is skipped for EFT models. Halofit/HMcode w0wa was already filed by t8c.  Python mods: - The P(k) extrapolation mod in results.py is about 10x less accurate than upstream beyond kmax, for every model including GR. - The theta-to-H0 solve fails for 4 of 73 test models, because the EFT model is unstable or unsolvable at the ends of the H0 bracket. For 5_scg a trial H0 of 100 hits an MpiStop that kills Python. - set_classes / set_dark_energy are silently ignored while an EFT model is active.  All S1/S2 repros were run in this session; numbers are quoted in the findings. Findings already filed by other tracks are referenced in the checks and not re-reported: t8c halofit w0wa, theta H0 range, ini theta-before-EFT, 21cm; t3/t9 set_params(cp=); t0 SetParams DarkEnergy check using the old CP; t10a forced IntTolBoost. Tree copies and bulky npz were deleted; diffs and summaries are kept in work/t8b-integration/out.

### t8c-features: FINDINGS

Track 8c tested the user-facing CAMB v2 features with EFT on. I listed them from CHANGELOG.md and git diff 1db4141..camb: AccuracyTarget=1, automatic lens_potential_accuracy/kmax, check_accuracy, lens_output_margin and the new lensing method, the SPk/ExternalNonLinearRatio/SecondOrderPK hooks, HMcode wiggle reuse, reionization heating, the new mnu/omnuh2_active/hierarchy neutrino setter, and theta (thetastar/cosmomc_theta) as ini keys. Models used: designer f(R) LCDM and wCDM, hdsk_TG, K-mouflage, exponential quintessence, PEFT gamma3/Omega, plus GR and near-GR controls.  Features that work with EFT on: - Auto lens accuracy does not under-resolve the MG models: auto vs lens_potential_accuracy=8 differs by less than 1e-3. - Number counts and lensing windows match GR in the near-GR limit to 1.6e-5. - The Weyl transfer uses the EFT lensing potential, and the transfer-only path is bit-identical to the full run. - The neutrino hierarchy setter gives the same answer as GR in the near-GR limit to 1.4e-5. - No dark-energy double counting. - get_age and get_zre_from_tau are consistent. - Python set_cosmology(thetastar or cosmomc_theta) is exact. - check_accuracy, ExternalNonLinearRatio, SecondOrderPK, SPk, reionization heating and the AccurateBB/lens margin options all run with EFT. - The AccuracyTarget default changes EFT results about as much as GR.  Six defects found, each with a repro I ran: 1. **S1:** thetastar/cosmomc_theta given in an ini are solved for H0 before EFTCAMB is initialised. H0 comes out wrong by 0.7–4% in theta for every model whose background is not LCDM. Fix tested: error drops to 1e-7. 2. **S2:** Halofit and HMcode always receive w0=-1, wa=0 for EFT models. P_nl/P_lin is 3–7.5% off in the no-MG limit. Fix tested: 5e-6. 3. **S2:** the v2 default set_for_lmax silently switches on GR-calibrated non-linear lensing for MG models (lensed BB changes by 35%). 4. **S2:** 21cm is refused only by the command-line driver; Python computes it silently with GR source substitutions. 5. **S2:** omk != 0 is silently wrong; this overlaps the t3 finding. 6. **S3:** problems with the H0 search range for the theta solver.  Already reported by other tracks, so not repeated here: - get_background_densities ignores the EFT background, and hdsk H0 is not propagated (t8b). - K-mouflage with massive neutrinos calls MpiStop and kills Python (stop-kills-python). - The theta solve aborts when a bracket end is unstable (t8b).  Not filed, because it was not caused by v2: low-ell TT of 2_PEFT_gamma3_const_1 and 5_Kmouflage_1 is not converged at the 1–2% level, and changes non-monotonically with time sampling. B1 shows the same size of error, and check_accuracy flags it.

| Check | Value | Threshold | Pass |

### t9-wrapper: FINDINGS

Track 9 audited the Python wrapper (camb/eftcamb.py <-> 11_EFTCAMB_python.f90, plus the EFT parts of camb.py, model.py, _ini.py and results.py) on tree A0. It found 1 S1, 8 S2 and 2 S3 issues. Every S1/S2 repro was run on A0. The candidate fixes were built in a tree copy (work/t9-wrapper/A0fix) and checked before and after.  What works: - (i) get_eft_functions background fields match the DEBUG cache dump to <=5e-11 (worst field 1.1e-7, hdsk EFTGamma2V) for 3 models. - get_scale_evolution equals get_evolution; multi-k equals single-k; a scalar input matches an array input to 7e-9. - pi is 0 before the turn-on, and tau(a=1) equals tau0. - (ii)/(iii) For all 73 test inis, read_parameters -> initialize_parameters and camb.set_params(**dict) give results bit-identical to read_ini, once bytes values are decoded. - (v) Bad model-selection numbers and unstable/NaN/inf parameters raise Python exceptions, and later runs match a fresh process exactly (0.0). - (vii) The only EFT/set_params key collision is 'cs2' (ADE). It is harmless: TT identical to the ini path. No CAMB key is read by EFTCAMB.  Defects: - S1: on a GR or failed-init object, num_params/param_names/param_labels/param_values segfault. A failed initialize_parameters leaves EFTflag set with no model, and get_results on that object then segfaults. - S2: a value Fortran cannot parse (EFTflag=1.0, np.True_, np.float64, None, bytes) hits ERROR STOP and kills the interpreter. - S2: read_parameters returns bytes for string values and for D-exponent numbers ('1.d-4'), so its own round trip kills Python on 4_Des_F_R_LCDM_2. - S2: set_params(cp=pars, ...) silently resets the EFT model to GR (TT off by 73% against the correct control). - S2: write_ini writes no EFT keys. Validation fails for 72/72 models; with validate=False the re-read model is GR. - S2: get_evolution / get_scale_evolution return rows sorted, not in the requested order. - S2: at the default tolerance, pi, Psi and mu from get_evolution at k~1/Mpc are dominated by integration error (14-32x the converged amplitude). - S2: EFTCAMB_turn_on_time = 0, negative, NaN or >1 silently gives GR perturbations, because the return-to-GR step maps the bad value to a=1.1. - S2: `import camb` fails for ifort/flang builds: the bind(c) symbol is looked up without its trailing underscore. - S3: F-001 follow-ups and assorted wrapper hygiene.  Fix tree: all repros pass on it, write_ini round-trips 72/72 models, and 6 regression models differ from A0 by <=5.6e-15.
